```yaml
      max-lifetime: 1740000
      connection-timeout: 30000
      validation-timeout: 5000
      keepalive-time: 60000
      connection-test-query: SELECT 1
      pool-name: ruoyi-hikari
    type: com.zaxxer.hikari.HikariDataSource
```

`ruoyi-redis-prod.yml`：

```yaml
spring:
  redis:
    host: <REDIS_ENDPOINT>
    port: 6379
    password: ${REDIS_PASSWORD}
    database: 0
    timeout: 3000ms
    lettuce:
      pool:
        max-active: 50
        max-idle: 20
        min-idle: 5
        max-wait: 2000ms
      shutdown-timeout: 200ms
```

`ruoyi-gateway-routes-prod.yml`（关键路由，须与 RuoYi 路由表一致）：

```yaml
spring:
  cloud:
    gateway:
      discovery:
        locator:
          enabled: false          # 生产关闭自动路由，避免暴露内部服务
      routes:
        - id: ruoyi-auth
          uri: lb://ruoyi-auth
          predicates:
            - Path=/auth/**
          filters:
            - StripPrefix=1
        - id: ruoyi-system
          uri: lb://ruoyi-modules-system
          predicates:
            - Path=/system/**
          filters:
            - StripPrefix=1
        - id: ruoyi-gen
          uri: lb://ruoyi-modules-gen
          predicates:
            - Path=/code/**
          filters:
            - StripPrefix=1
        - id: ruoyi-job
          uri: lb://ruoyi-modules-job
          predicates:
            - Path=/schedule/**
          filters:
            - StripPrefix=1
        - id: ruoyi-file
          uri: lb://ruoyi-modules-file
          predicates:
            - Path=/file/**
          filters:
            - StripPrefix=1
    sentinel:
      eager: true
      transport:
        dashboard: sentinel-dashboard.ruoyi-middleware.svc.cluster.local:8858
        port: 8719
      datasource:
        flow:
          nacos:
            server-addr: ruoyi-nacos.ruoyi-middleware.svc.cluster.local:8848
            namespace: ruoyi-prod
            group-id: RUOYI_SENTINEL_GROUP
            data-id: sentinel-ruoyi-gateway-gw-flow-rules
            data-type: json
            rule-type: gw-flow
        degrade:
          nacos:
            server-addr: ruoyi-nacos.ruoyi-middleware.svc.cluster.local:8848
            namespace: ruoyi-prod
            group-id: RUOYI_SENTINEL_GROUP
            data-id: sentinel-ruoyi-gateway-degrade-rules
            data-type: json
            rule-type: degrade
      scg:
        fallback:
          mode: response
          response-status: 429
          response-body: '{"code":429,"msg":"请求过于频繁，请稍后重试"}'
```

#### 4.2.5 敏感配置加密建议

| 方案 | 实现 | 推荐度 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 环境变量 + Secret | 配置里写 `${RDS_PASSWORD}`，值来自 K8s Secret | ★★★★★ | 本手册采用，简单可靠 |
| 阿里云 KMS 加密 + 应用解密 | 配置中存密文，应用启动时调 KMS 解密 | ★★★★ | 适合多环境共享配置；需引入 SDK |
| Jasypt 加密 | `ENC(...)` + 启动传入 master key | ★★★ | 密钥管理仍是问题 |
| MSE 配置加密 | 使用 MSE 加密配置功能 | ★★★★ | 依赖 MSE |
| RRSA 免 AK | OSS/SLS 等云资源不落 AK | ★★★★★ | 与 Secret 方案互补 |

> **底线要求**：任何 Secret（密码/密钥）**禁止**明文写入 Nacos 配置、Git 仓库、镜像层。Nacos 中只允许出现 `${VAR}` 占位。

#### 4.2.6 配置版本与灰度发布策略

| 策略 | 做法 | 适用 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 版本留痕 | 每次配置变更需填 `变更单号-日期` 到配置描述字段 | 全部 |
| 双份配置 | `ruoyi-gateway-prod.yml`（稳定）与 `ruoyi-gateway-gray.yml`（灰度） | 灰度期 |
| Nacos 历史回滚 | 控制台「历史版本」→ 回滚 | 配置错误应急 |
| Beta 发布 | 配置 → 编辑 → 勾选「Beta 发布」→ 指定 IP | 单实例验证 |
| 标签路由 | `spring.cloud.nacos.discovery.metadata.version=canary` + 网关按 metadata 路由 | 金丝雀 |
| 灰度顺序 | 配置 → 1 个 Pod（Beta）→ 全量 | 全部 |

配置变更 SOP：

```text
1. 在 Nacos 控制台「配置管理」→ 新建/编辑 → 保存（不立即发布）
2. 填写变更单号到描述 → 记录变更前后内容（diff 存档）
3. 使用「Beta 发布」指定 1 个 Pod IP → 观察 5-10 分钟指标
4. 通过 → 「发布」全量；不通过 → 「历史版本」→ 回滚
5. 变更后 30 分钟内保持监控大屏可见，值班人不得离岗
```

### 4.3 部署清单编写

#### 4.3.1 统一标签规范

```yaml
labels:
  app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-gateway        # 服务名（唯一）
  app.kubernetes.io/instance: ruoyi-gateway-prod  # 实例（环境相关）
  app.kubernetes.io/version: "3.6.5"           # 版本
  app.kubernetes.io/component: api-gateway     # 组件类型
  app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud       # 所属系统（统一）
  app.kubernetes.io/managed-by: Helm           # 管理方式（统一）
```

组件类型取值表：

| 服务 | component |
| --- | --- |
| ruoyi-gateway | `api-gateway` |
| ruoyi-auth | `auth-service` |
| ruoyi-modules-system | `business-service` |
| ruoyi-modules-gen | `business-service` |
| ruoyi-modules-job | `scheduler` |
| ruoyi-modules-file | `file-service` |
| ruoyi-nginx | `frontend` |
| ruoyi-nacos | `registry-config` |
| ruoyi-sentinel-dashboard | `flow-control` |
| ruoyi-skywalking-oap | `apm-backend` |
| ruoyi-elasticsearch | `log-storage` |
| ruoyi-prometheus | `metrics` |

#### 4.3.2 ruoyi-gateway 完整清单（可直接 apply）

`deploy/k8s/20-gateway.yaml`：

```yaml
# ==================== ServiceAccount ====================
apiVersion: v1
kind: ServiceAccount
metadata:
  name: ruoyi-gateway
  namespace: ruoyi-prod
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-gateway
    app.kubernetes.io/instance: ruoyi-gateway-prod
    app.kubernetes.io/version: "3.6.5"
    app.kubernetes.io/component: api-gateway
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
    app.kubernetes.io/managed-by: Helm
  annotations:
    ack.alibabacloud.com/enabled: "true"
    ack.alibabacloud.com/role-arn: "acs:ram::<YOUR_ACCOUNT_ID>:role/ruoyi-prod-pod-role"
imagePullSecrets:
  - name: ruoyi-acr-secret
---
# ==================== ConfigMap ====================
apiVersion: v1
kind: ConfigMap
metadata:
  name: ruoyi-gateway-config
  namespace: ruoyi-prod
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-gateway
    app.kubernetes.io/instance: ruoyi-gateway-prod
    app.kubernetes.io/version: "3.6.5"
    app.kubernetes.io/component: api-gateway
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
    app.kubernetes.io/managed-by: Helm
data:
  SPRING_PROFILES_ACTIVE: "prod"
  NACOS_ADDR: "ruoyi-nacos.ruoyi-middleware.svc.cluster.local:8848"
  NACOS_NAMESPACE: "ruoyi-prod"
  NACOS_GROUP: "DEFAULT_GROUP"
  APP_VERSION: "3.6.5"
  SERVER_PORT: "8080"
  SW_AGENT_COLLECTOR_BACKEND_SERVICES: "skywalking-oap.ruoyi-monitoring.svc.cluster.local:11800"
  SW_AGENT_NAME: "ruoyi-gateway"
  SW_SAMPLE_N_PER_3_SECS: "5000"
  # JVM_XMX ≤ 容器 memory limit 的 75%（预留 metaspace/线程栈/直接内存）；JVM_XMS = requests
  JVM_XMS: "1g"
  JVM_XMX: "1536m"
  JVM_XMN: "512m"
  TZ: "Asia/Shanghai"
---
# ==================== Service ====================
apiVersion: v1
kind: Service
metadata:
  name: ruoyi-gateway
  namespace: ruoyi-prod
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-gateway
    app.kubernetes.io/instance: ruoyi-gateway-prod
    app.kubernetes.io/version: "3.6.5"
    app.kubernetes.io/component: api-gateway
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
    app.kubernetes.io/managed-by: Helm
spec:
  type: ClusterIP
  ports:
    - name: http
      port: 8080
      targetPort: http
      protocol: TCP
  selector:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-gateway
    app.kubernetes.io/instance: ruoyi-gateway-prod
---
# ==================== Deployment ====================
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: ruoyi-gateway
  namespace: ruoyi-prod
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-gateway
    app.kubernetes.io/instance: ruoyi-gateway-prod
    app.kubernetes.io/version: "3.6.5"
    app.kubernetes.io/component: api-gateway
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
    app.kubernetes.io/managed-by: Helm
  annotations:
    deployment.kubernetes.io/revision: "1"
    kubernetes.io/change-cause: "initial deploy v3.6.5"
spec:
  replicas: 3
  revisionHistoryLimit: 5
  progressDeadlineSeconds: 600
  minReadySeconds: 30
  strategy:
    type: RollingUpdate
    rollingUpdate:
      maxSurge: 1
      maxUnavailable: 0
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-gateway
      app.kubernetes.io/instance: ruoyi-gateway-prod
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-gateway
        app.kubernetes.io/instance: ruoyi-gateway-prod
        app.kubernetes.io/version: "3.6.5"
        app.kubernetes.io/component: api-gateway
        app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
        app.kubernetes.io/managed-by: Helm
      annotations:
        prometheus.io/scrape: "true"
        prometheus.io/port: "8080"
        prometheus.io/path: "/actuator/prometheus"
        sidecar.istio.io/inject: "false"
    spec:
      serviceAccountName: ruoyi-gateway
      imagePullSecrets:
        - name: ruoyi-acr-secret
      terminationGracePeriodSeconds: 60
      securityContext:
        runAsNonRoot: true
        runAsUser: 1000
        runAsGroup: 1000
        fsGroup: 1000
        seccompProfile:
          type: RuntimeDefault
      # ---------- 拓扑打散：3 AZ ----------
      topologySpreadConstraints:
        - maxSkew: 1
          topologyKey: topology.kubernetes.io/zone
          whenUnsatisfiable: DoNotSchedule
          labelSelector:
            matchLabels:
              app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-gateway
              app.kubernetes.io/instance: ruoyi-gateway-prod
        - maxSkew: 1
          topologyKey: kubernetes.io/hostname
          whenUnsatisfiable: ScheduleAnyway
          labelSelector:
            matchLabels:
              app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-gateway
      affinity:
        nodeAffinity:
          preferredDuringSchedulingIgnoredDuringExecution:
            - weight: 100
              preference:
                matchExpressions:
                  - key: node-pool
                    operator: In
                    values: ["general"]
        podAntiAffinity:
          preferredDuringSchedulingIgnoredDuringExecution:
            - weight: 100
              podAffinityTerm:
                topologyKey: kubernetes.io/hostname
                labelSelector:
                  matchLabels:
                    app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-gateway
      tolerations:
        - key: dedicated
          operator: Exists
          effect: NoSchedule
      # ---------- initContainer：等待 Nacos 就绪 ----------
      initContainers:
        - name: wait-for-nacos
          image: <YOUR_ACR_REGISTRY>/ruoyi-prod/rocky-tools:9.3
          imagePullPolicy: IfNotPresent
          command: ["/bin/sh", "-c"]
          args:
            - |
              set -e
              ADDR="${NACOS_ADDR%%:*}"
              PORT="${NACOS_ADDR##*:}"
              echo "waiting for Nacos ${ADDR}:${PORT} ..."
              for i in $(seq 1 120); do
                if nc -z "$ADDR" "$PORT" 2>/dev/null; then
                  echo "Nacos port reachable after ${i} tries"
                  # 进一步校验 HTTP 健康
                  if curl -fsS -m 3 "http://${NACOS_ADDR}/nacos/actuator/health" | grep -q '"status":"UP"'; then
                    echo "Nacos health UP"; exit 0
                  fi
                fi
                sleep 3
              done
              echo "FATAL: Nacos not ready after 360s"; exit 1
          envFrom:
            - configMapRef:
                name: ruoyi-gateway-config
          resources:
            requests: { cpu: 10m, memory: 32Mi }
            limits: { cpu: 100m, memory: 64Mi }
          securityContext:
            allowPrivilegeEscalation: false
            readOnlyRootFilesystem: true
            runAsNonRoot: true
            runAsUser: 1000
            capabilities: { drop: ["ALL"] }
        - name: fetch-skywalking-agent
          image: <YOUR_ACR_REGISTRY>/ruoyi-prod/rocky-tools:9.3
          imagePullPolicy: IfNotPresent
          command: ["/bin/sh", "-c"]
          args:
            - |
              set -e
              if [ -f /agent/skywalking-agent.jar ]; then
                echo "agent already present, skip"; exit 0
              fi
              echo "downloading skywalking agent 9.4.0 ..."
              curl -fsSL -o /tmp/agent.tgz "https://<OSS_BUCKET>.oss-cn-hangzhou-internal.aliyuncs.com/agent/skywalking-agent-9.4.0.tgz"
              tar -xzf /tmp/agent.tgz -C /tmp
              cp -r /tmp/skywalking-agent/. /agent/
              echo "agent ready"
          resources:
            requests: { cpu: 10m, memory: 64Mi }
            limits: { cpu: 200m, memory: 256Mi }
          securityContext:
            allowPrivilegeEscalation: false
            runAsNonRoot: true
            runAsUser: 1000
            capabilities: { drop: ["ALL"] }
          volumeMounts:
            - name: skywalking-agent
              mountPath: /agent
      containers:
        - name: ruoyi-gateway
          image: <YOUR_ACR_REGISTRY>/ruoyi-prod/ruoyi-gateway:3.6.5
          imagePullPolicy: IfNotPresent
          ports:
            - name: http
              containerPort: 8080
              protocol: TCP
          envFrom:
            - configMapRef:
                name: ruoyi-gateway-config
          env:
            - name: NACOS_PASSWORD
              valueFrom:
                secretKeyRef:
                  name: ruoyi-runtime-secret
                  key: nacos-password
            - name: NACOS_USERNAME
              value: "nacos"
            - name: POD_NAME
              valueFrom:
                fieldRef:
                  fieldPath: metadata.name
            - name: POD_IP
              valueFrom:
                fieldRef:
                  fieldPath: status.podIP
            - name: NODE_NAME
              valueFrom:
                fieldRef:
                  fieldPath: spec.nodeName
            - name: NODE_ZONE
              valueFrom:
                fieldRef:
                  fieldPath: metadata.labels['topology.kubernetes.io/zone']
          resources:
            requests:
              cpu: 500m
              memory: 1Gi
            limits:
              cpu: "2"
              memory: 2Gi
          # ---------- 三类探针 ----------
          startupProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /actuator/health
              port: http
            initialDelaySeconds: 30
            periodSeconds: 5
            timeoutSeconds: 3
            failureThreshold: 36          # 最长 210s 启动窗口
          readinessProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /actuator/health/readiness
              port: http
            initialDelaySeconds: 10
            periodSeconds: 10
            timeoutSeconds: 3
            successThreshold: 1
            failureThreshold: 3
          livenessProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /actuator/health/liveness
              port: http
            initialDelaySeconds: 60
            periodSeconds: 15
            timeoutSeconds: 3
            failureThreshold: 4
          lifecycle:
            preStop:
              exec:
                command:
                  - /bin/sh
                  - -c
                  - |
                    echo "preStop: deregister from Nacos and drain connections"
                    curl -s -X POST "http://127.0.0.1:8080/actuator/shutdown" || true
                    sleep 20
          securityContext:
            allowPrivilegeEscalation: false
            readOnlyRootFilesystem: false
            runAsNonRoot: true
            runAsUser: 1000
            capabilities:
              drop: ["ALL"]
          volumeMounts:
            - name: skywalking-agent
              mountPath: /app/skywalking-agent
              readOnly: true
            - name: logs
              mountPath: /app/logs
            - name: tmp
              mountPath: /tmp
      volumes:
        - name: skywalking-agent
          emptyDir:
            sizeLimit: 200Mi
        - name: logs
          emptyDir:
            sizeLimit: 2Gi
        - name: tmp
          emptyDir:
            sizeLimit: 256Mi
---
# ==================== HPA ====================
apiVersion: autoscaling/v2
kind: HorizontalPodAutoscaler
metadata:
  name: ruoyi-gateway
  namespace: ruoyi-prod
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-gateway
    app.kubernetes.io/instance: ruoyi-gateway-prod
    app.kubernetes.io/version: "3.6.5"
    app.kubernetes.io/component: api-gateway
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
    app.kubernetes.io/managed-by: Helm
spec:
  scaleTargetRef:
    apiVersion: apps/v1
    kind: Deployment
    name: ruoyi-gateway
  minReplicas: 3
  maxReplicas: 9
  metrics:
    - type: Resource
      resource:
        name: cpu
        target:
          type: Utilization
          averageUtilization: 70
    - type: Resource
      resource:
        name: memory
        target:
          type: Utilization
          averageUtilization: 80
  behavior:
    scaleUp:
      stabilizationWindowSeconds: 60
      policies:
        - type: Percent
          value: 100
          periodSeconds: 60
        - type: Pods
          value: 2
          periodSeconds: 60
      selectPolicy: Max
    scaleDown:
      stabilizationWindowSeconds: 300
      policies:
        - type: Percent
          value: 25
          periodSeconds: 60
      selectPolicy: Min
---
# ==================== PDB ====================
apiVersion: policy/v1
kind: PodDisruptionBudget
metadata:
  name: ruoyi-gateway
  namespace: ruoyi-prod
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-gateway
    app.kubernetes.io/instance: ruoyi-gateway-prod
    app.kubernetes.io/version: "3.6.5"
    app.kubernetes.io/component: api-gateway
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
    app.kubernetes.io/managed-by: Helm
spec:
  minAvailable: 2
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-gateway
      app.kubernetes.io/instance: ruoyi-gateway-prod
```

依赖 Secret（Nacos 密码等）：

```bash
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod create secret generic ruoyi-runtime-secret \
  --from-literal=nacos-password='<NACOS_PASSWORD>' \
  --from-literal=redis-password='<REDIS_PASSWORD>' \
  --from-literal=rds-password='<RDS_PASSWORD>' \
  --from-literal=token-secret="$(openssl rand -hex 32)"
```

```bash
kubectl apply -f deploy/k8s/20-gateway.yaml
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod rollout status deploy/ruoyi-gateway --timeout=600s
# 预期: deployment "ruoyi-gateway" successfully rolled out
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod get pods -l app.kubernetes.io/name=ruoyi-gateway -o wide
# 预期: 3 个 Pod，Running，分布在不同 AZ
```

#### 4.3.3 ruoyi-modules-system 完整清单（可直接 apply）

`deploy/k8s/21-system.yaml`：

```yaml
# ==================== ServiceAccount ====================
apiVersion: v1
kind: ServiceAccount
metadata:
  name: ruoyi-modules-system
  namespace: ruoyi-prod
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-modules-system
    app.kubernetes.io/instance: ruoyi-modules-system-prod
    app.kubernetes.io/version: "3.6.5"
    app.kubernetes.io/component: business-service
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
    app.kubernetes.io/managed-by: Helm
  annotations:
    ack.alibabacloud.com/enabled: "true"
    ack.alibabacloud.com/role-arn: "acs:ram::<YOUR_ACCOUNT_ID>:role/ruoyi-prod-pod-role"
imagePullSecrets:
  - name: ruoyi-acr-secret
---
# ==================== ConfigMap ====================
apiVersion: v1
kind: ConfigMap
metadata:
  name: ruoyi-modules-system-config
  namespace: ruoyi-prod
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-modules-system
    app.kubernetes.io/instance: ruoyi-modules-system-prod
    app.kubernetes.io/version: "3.6.5"
    app.kubernetes.io/component: business-service
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
    app.kubernetes.io/managed-by: Helm
data:
  SPRING_PROFILES_ACTIVE: "prod"
  NACOS_ADDR: "ruoyi-nacos.ruoyi-middleware.svc.cluster.local:8848"
  NACOS_NAMESPACE: "ruoyi-prod"
  NACOS_GROUP: "DEFAULT_GROUP"
  APP_VERSION: "3.6.5"
  SERVER_PORT: "9201"
  SW_AGENT_COLLECTOR_BACKEND_SERVICES: "skywalking-oap.ruoyi-monitoring.svc.cluster.local:11800"
  SW_AGENT_NAME: "ruoyi-modules-system"
  SW_SAMPLE_N_PER_3_SECS: "5000"
  # JVM_XMX ≤ 容器 memory limit 的 75%（预留 metaspace/线程栈/直接内存）；JVM_XMS = requests
  JVM_XMS: "2g"
  JVM_XMX: "3g"
  JVM_XMN: "1g"
  TZ: "Asia/Shanghai"
---
# ==================== Service ====================
apiVersion: v1
kind: Service
metadata:
  name: ruoyi-modules-system
  namespace: ruoyi-prod
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-modules-system
    app.kubernetes.io/instance: ruoyi-modules-system-prod
    app.kubernetes.io/version: "3.6.5"
    app.kubernetes.io/component: business-service
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
    app.kubernetes.io/managed-by: Helm
spec:
  type: ClusterIP
  ports:
    - name: http
      port: 9201
      targetPort: http
      protocol: TCP
  selector:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-modules-system
    app.kubernetes.io/instance: ruoyi-modules-system-prod
---
# ==================== Deployment ====================
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: ruoyi-modules-system
  namespace: ruoyi-prod
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-modules-system
    app.kubernetes.io/instance: ruoyi-modules-system-prod
    app.kubernetes.io/version: "3.6.5"
    app.kubernetes.io/component: business-service
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
    app.kubernetes.io/managed-by: Helm
  annotations:
    kubernetes.io/change-cause: "initial deploy v3.6.5"
spec:
  replicas: 3
  revisionHistoryLimit: 5
  progressDeadlineSeconds: 600
  minReadySeconds: 30
  strategy:
    type: RollingUpdate
    rollingUpdate:
      maxSurge: 1
      maxUnavailable: 0
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-modules-system
      app.kubernetes.io/instance: ruoyi-modules-system-prod
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-modules-system
        app.kubernetes.io/instance: ruoyi-modules-system-prod
        app.kubernetes.io/version: "3.6.5"
        app.kubernetes.io/component: business-service
        app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
        app.kubernetes.io/managed-by: Helm
      annotations:
        prometheus.io/scrape: "true"
        prometheus.io/port: "9201"
        prometheus.io/path: "/actuator/prometheus"
    spec:
      serviceAccountName: ruoyi-modules-system
      imagePullSecrets:
        - name: ruoyi-acr-secret
      terminationGracePeriodSeconds: 90
      securityContext:
        runAsNonRoot: true
        runAsUser: 1000
        runAsGroup: 1000
        fsGroup: 1000
        seccompProfile:
          type: RuntimeDefault
      topologySpreadConstraints:
        - maxSkew: 1
          topologyKey: topology.kubernetes.io/zone
          whenUnsatisfiable: DoNotSchedule
          labelSelector:
            matchLabels:
              app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-modules-system
              app.kubernetes.io/instance: ruoyi-modules-system-prod
      affinity:
        nodeAffinity:
          preferredDuringSchedulingIgnoredDuringExecution:
            - weight: 100
              preference:
                matchExpressions:
                  - key: node-pool
                    operator: In
                    values: ["general"]
        podAntiAffinity:
          preferredDuringSchedulingIgnoredDuringExecution:
            - weight: 100
              podAffinityTerm:
                topologyKey: kubernetes.io/hostname
                labelSelector:
                  matchLabels:
                    app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-modules-system
      tolerations:
        - key: dedicated
          operator: Exists
          effect: NoSchedule
      initContainers:
        - name: wait-for-nacos
          image: <YOUR_ACR_REGISTRY>/ruoyi-prod/rocky-tools:9.3
          command: ["/bin/sh", "-c"]
          args:
            - |
              set -e
              ADDR="${NACOS_ADDR%%:*}"; PORT="${NACOS_ADDR##*:}"
              echo "waiting for Nacos ${ADDR}:${PORT} ..."
              for i in $(seq 1 120); do
                if nc -z "$ADDR" "$PORT" 2>/dev/null && \
                   curl -fsS -m 3 "http://${NACOS_ADDR}/nacos/actuator/health" | grep -q '"status":"UP"'; then
                  echo "Nacos ready after ${i} tries"; exit 0
                fi
                sleep 3
              done
              echo "FATAL: Nacos not ready after 360s"; exit 1
          envFrom:
            - configMapRef:
                name: ruoyi-modules-system-config
          env:
            - name: NACOS_PASSWORD
              valueFrom:
                secretKeyRef:
                  name: ruoyi-runtime-secret
                  key: nacos-password
            - name: NACOS_USERNAME
              value: "nacos"
          resources:
            requests: { cpu: 10m, memory: 32Mi }
            limits: { cpu: 100m, memory: 64Mi }
          securityContext:
            allowPrivilegeEscalation: false
            readOnlyRootFilesystem: true
            runAsNonRoot: true
            runAsUser: 1000
            capabilities: { drop: ["ALL"] }
        - name: wait-for-mysql
          image: <YOUR_ACR_REGISTRY>/ruoyi-prod/rocky-tools:9.3
          command: ["/bin/sh", "-c"]
          args:
            - |
              echo "waiting for MySQL <RDS_ENDPOINT>:3306 ..."
              for i in $(seq 1 60); do
                nc -z <RDS_ENDPOINT> 3306 && { echo "MySQL reachable"; exit 0; }
                sleep 3
              done
              echo "FATAL: MySQL not reachable"; exit 1
          resources:
            requests: { cpu: 10m, memory: 32Mi }
            limits: { cpu: 100m, memory: 64Mi }
          securityContext:
            allowPrivilegeEscalation: false
            readOnlyRootFilesystem: true
            runAsNonRoot: true
            runAsUser: 1000
            capabilities: { drop: ["ALL"] }
        - name: fetch-skywalking-agent
          image: <YOUR_ACR_REGISTRY>/ruoyi-prod/rocky-tools:9.3
          command: ["/bin/sh", "-c"]
          args:
            - |
              set -e
              [ -f /agent/skywalking-agent.jar ] && { echo "agent present"; exit 0; }
              curl -fsSL -o /tmp/agent.tgz "https://<OSS_BUCKET>.oss-cn-hangzhou-internal.aliyuncs.com/agent/skywalking-agent-9.4.0.tgz"
              tar -xzf /tmp/agent.tgz -C /tmp && cp -r /tmp/skywalking-agent/. /agent/
              echo "agent ready"
          resources:
            requests: { cpu: 10m, memory: 64Mi }
            limits: { cpu: 200m, memory: 256Mi }
          securityContext:
            allowPrivilegeEscalation: false
            runAsNonRoot: true
            runAsUser: 1000
            capabilities: { drop: ["ALL"] }
          volumeMounts:
            - name: skywalking-agent
              mountPath: /agent
      containers:
        - name: ruoyi-modules-system
          image: <YOUR_ACR_REGISTRY>/ruoyi-prod/ruoyi-modules-system:3.6.5
          imagePullPolicy: IfNotPresent
          ports:
            - name: http
              containerPort: 9201
              protocol: TCP
          envFrom:
            - configMapRef:
                name: ruoyi-modules-system-config
          env:
            - name: NACOS_PASSWORD
              valueFrom:
                secretKeyRef:
                  name: ruoyi-runtime-secret
                  key: nacos-password
            - name: NACOS_USERNAME
              value: "nacos"
            - name: RDS_PASSWORD
              valueFrom:
                secretKeyRef:
                  name: ruoyi-runtime-secret
                  key: rds-password
            - name: REDIS_PASSWORD
              valueFrom:
                secretKeyRef:
                  name: ruoyi-runtime-secret
                  key: redis-password
            - name: POD_NAME
              valueFrom:
                fieldRef:
                  fieldPath: metadata.name
            - name: NODE_ZONE
              valueFrom:
                fieldRef:
                  fieldPath: metadata.labels['topology.kubernetes.io/zone']
          resources:
            requests:
              cpu: 1000m
              memory: 2Gi
            limits:
              cpu: "2"
              memory: 4Gi
          startupProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /actuator/health
              port: http
            initialDelaySeconds: 30
            periodSeconds: 5
            timeoutSeconds: 3
            failureThreshold: 36
          readinessProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /actuator/health/readiness
              port: http
            initialDelaySeconds: 10
            periodSeconds: 10
            timeoutSeconds: 3
            failureThreshold: 3
          livenessProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /actuator/health/liveness
              port: http
            initialDelaySeconds: 60
            periodSeconds: 15
            timeoutSeconds: 3
            failureThreshold: 4
          lifecycle:
            preStop:
              exec:
                command:
                  - /bin/sh
                  - -c
                  - |
                    echo "preStop: deregister and drain"
                    curl -s -X POST "http://127.0.0.1:9201/actuator/shutdown" || true
                    sleep 25
          securityContext:
            allowPrivilegeEscalation: false
            readOnlyRootFilesystem: false
            runAsNonRoot: true
            runAsUser: 1000
            capabilities:
              drop: ["ALL"]
          volumeMounts:
            - name: skywalking-agent
              mountPath: /app/skywalking-agent
              readOnly: true
            - name: logs
              mountPath: /app/logs
            - name: upload
              mountPath: /data/upload
            - name: tmp
              mountPath: /tmp
      volumes:
        - name: skywalking-agent
          emptyDir:
            sizeLimit: 200Mi
        - name: logs
          emptyDir:
            sizeLimit: 2Gi
        - name: upload
          persistentVolumeClaim:
            claimName: ruoyi-upload-pvc
        - name: tmp
          emptyDir:
            sizeLimit: 256Mi
---
# ==================== HPA ====================
apiVersion: autoscaling/v2
kind: HorizontalPodAutoscaler
metadata:
  name: ruoyi-modules-system
  namespace: ruoyi-prod
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-modules-system
    app.kubernetes.io/instance: ruoyi-modules-system-prod
    app.kubernetes.io/version: "3.6.5"
    app.kubernetes.io/component: business-service
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
    app.kubernetes.io/managed-by: Helm
spec:
  scaleTargetRef:
    apiVersion: apps/v1
    kind: Deployment
    name: ruoyi-modules-system
  minReplicas: 3
  maxReplicas: 9
  metrics:
    - type: Resource
      resource:
        name: cpu
        target: { type: Utilization, averageUtilization: 70 }
    - type: Resource
      resource:
        name: memory
        target: { type: Utilization, averageUtilization: 80 }
  behavior:
    scaleUp:
      stabilizationWindowSeconds: 60
      policies:
        - type: Percent
          value: 100
          periodSeconds: 60
      selectPolicy: Max
    scaleDown:
      stabilizationWindowSeconds: 300
      policies:
        - type: Percent
          value: 25
          periodSeconds: 60
      selectPolicy: Min
---
# ==================== PDB ====================
apiVersion: policy/v1
kind: PodDisruptionBudget
metadata:
  name: ruoyi-modules-system
  namespace: ruoyi-prod
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-modules-system
    app.kubernetes.io/instance: ruoyi-modules-system-prod
    app.kubernetes.io/version: "3.6.5"
    app.kubernetes.io/component: business-service
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
    app.kubernetes.io/managed-by: Helm
spec:
  minAvailable: 2
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-modules-system
      app.kubernetes.io/instance: ruoyi-modules-system-prod
```

依赖的上传目录 PVC（NAS）：

```yaml
apiVersion: v1
kind: PersistentVolumeClaim
metadata:
  name: ruoyi-upload-pvc
  namespace: ruoyi-prod
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-upload
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
spec:
  accessModes: ["ReadWriteMany"]
  storageClassName: alicloud-nas-subpath
  resources:
    requests:
      storage: 100Gi
```

#### 4.3.4 其余 4 个服务：差异表与片段

| 服务 | 端口 | 副本 | requests/limits | HPA min/max | PDB | 特殊挂载 | 特殊配置 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `ruoyi-auth` | 9200 | 3 | 500m/1Gi → 2/2Gi | 3/9 | minAvailable 2 | 无 | JWT `token.secret` 来自 Secret |
| `ruoyi-modules-gen` | 9202 | 2 | 500m/1Gi → 1/2Gi | 2/4 | minAvailable 1 | 无 | 代码生成模板路径需挂载 |
| `ruoyi-modules-job` | 9203 | 2 | 500m/1Gi → 1/2Gi | 2/4 | minAvailable 1 | 无 | **Quartz 集群模式必须开启**，`org.quartz.jobStore.isClustered=true` |
| `ruoyi-modules-file` | 9300 | 2 | 500m/1Gi → 1/2Gi | 2/4 | minAvailable 1 | `ruoyi-upload-pvc` | OSS 参数、`domain` 配置 |

**ruoyi-auth 差异片段**：

```yaml
          env:
            - name: TOKEN_SECRET
              valueFrom:
                secretKeyRef:
                  name: ruoyi-runtime-secret
                  key: token-secret
          resources:
            requests: { cpu: 500m, memory: 1Gi }
            limits:   { cpu: "2", memory: 2Gi }
```

**ruoyi-modules-job 差异片段（关键）**：

```yaml
data:
  SERVER_PORT: "9203"
  # Quartz 集群模式：多副本必须开启，避免同一任务被多实例重复执行
  QUARTZ_IS_CLUSTERED: "true"
  QUARTZ_INSTANCE_ID: "AUTO"
  QUARTZ_CLUSTER_CHECKIN_INTERVAL: "20000"
  QUARTZ_CLUSTER_CHECKIN_MISFIRE_THRESHOLD: "60000"
  QUARTZ_TABLE_PREFIX: "QRTZ_"
```

对应 Nacos 配置 `ruoyi-modules-job-prod.yml`：

```yaml
spring:
  quartz:
    job-store-type: jdbc
    jdbc:
      initialize-schema: never        # 生产禁止自动建表，表由 quartz.sql 预先创建
    properties:
      org:
        quartz:
          scheduler:
            instanceName: ruoyiClusteredScheduler
            instanceId: AUTO          # 集群模式必须 AUTO
          jobStore:
            class: org.springframework.scheduling.quartz.LocalDataSourceJobStore
            driverDelegateClass: org.quartz.impl.jdbcjobstore.StdJDBCDelegate
            tablePrefix: QRTZ_
            isClustered: true          # ← 多副本必需
            clusterCheckinInterval: 20000
            misfireThreshold: 60000
          threadPool:
            class: org.quartz.simpl.SimpleThreadPool
            threadCount: 20
            threadPriority: 5
```

> **坑**：`instanceId` 若为固定值（如 `AUTO` 之外的自定义值），3 个副本会用同一个 instanceId，Quartz 集群表会互相覆盖，导致**任务重复执行或永不触发**。必须为 `AUTO`。

**ruoyi-modules-file 差异片段**：

```yaml
data:
  SERVER_PORT: "9300"
  FILE_UPLOAD_PATH: "/data/upload"
  FILE_DOMAIN: "https://api.ruoyi.example.com/statics"
```

**ruoyi-modules-gen 差异片段**：

```yaml
data:
  SERVER_PORT: "9202"
  GEN_TEMPLATE_PATH: "classpath:vm"
```

**生成其余服务的快速方法**（避免手工复制出错）：

```bash
# 以 system 为模板，批量替换生成其余服务的清单（替换后仍需人工复核）
for S in auth:9200:3 gen:9202:2 job:9203:2 file:9300:2; do
  NAME=ruoyi-modules-$(echo $S | cut -d: -f1)
  PORT=$(echo $S | cut -d: -f2)
  REP=$(echo $S | cut -d: -f3)
  sed -e "s/ruoyi-modules-system/${NAME}/g" \
      -e "s/9201/${PORT}/g" \
      -e "s/^  replicas: 3/  replicas: ${REP}/" \
      deploy/k8s/21-system.yaml > deploy/k8s/2x-${NAME}.yaml
  echo "generated deploy/k8s/2x-${NAME}.yaml (port=$PORT replicas=$REP) —— 请人工复核 resources/PDB/挂载"
done
```

#### 4.3.5 JVM 参数与容器内存的配比规则

**统一规则**：

```text
JVM_XMX = min(容器 limits.memory × 75%, 承载得住的实测峰值)
JVM_XMS = 容器 requests.memory
```

- 堆**不能**等于容器 `limits.memory`：`-Xmx` 一旦占满 limit，Metaspace、线程栈、直接内存（NIO/SkyWalking/Netty）、CodeCache 就无处可放，容器必然被 cgroup `OOMKilled`（Exit Code 137），且 `-XX:+ExitOnOutOfMemoryError` 也救不回来。
- 必须为堆外内存预留 **≥ 25%**，即 `JVM_XMX ≤ limits.memory × 75%`；`JVM_XMS` 取 `requests.memory`，避免堆动态伸缩带来的长停顿，也让 QoS 与调度预期一致。
- 若实测峰值低于 75% 上限，取实测峰值（扩容前不要盲目顶格设置）；`JVM_XMN` 取 `JVM_XMX` 的 1/3 ~ 1/2。

**6 个业务微服务的 requests/limits 与建议 JVM 参数对照表**（`requests(cpu/memory) → limits(cpu/memory)`）：

| 服务 | requests → limits | JVM_XMS | JVM_XMX | JVM_XMN | 堆占 limit |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `ruoyi-gateway` | 500m/1Gi → 2/2Gi | `1g` | `1536m`（≤75%） | `512m` | 75% |
| `ruoyi-auth` | 500m/1Gi → 2/2Gi | `1g` | `1536m`（≤75%） | `512m` | 75% |
| `ruoyi-modules-system` | 1000m/2Gi → 2/4Gi | `2g` | `3g`（≤75%） | `1g` | 75% |
| `ruoyi-modules-gen` | 500m/1Gi → 1/2Gi | `1g` | `1536m`（≤75%） | `512m` | 75% |
| `ruoyi-modules-job` | 500m/1Gi → 1/2Gi | `1g` | `1536m`（≤75%） | `512m` | 75% |
| `ruoyi-modules-file` | 500m/1Gi → 1/2Gi | `1g` | `1536m`（≤75%） | `512m` | 75% |

> 未显式设置 `JVM_XMX` 时由 `entrypoint.sh` 按容器内存自动计算（见 4.1 节，默认取 limit 的 70%，同样满足 ≤ 75% 约束）；ConfigMap 中显式配置了 `JVM_XMS/JVM_XMX/JVM_XMN` 的服务（如 4.3.2 的 gateway、4.3.3 的 system）以显式值为准，但**不得突破上表上限**。镜像 Dockerfile 的 ENV 默认值为 `JVM_XMS=1g / JVM_XMX=1536m / JVM_XMN=512m`（= 2Gi limit 的 75%，即上表 auth/gen/job/file 的取值），需要更大堆的服务由 ConfigMap 覆盖（system 为 `2g/3g/1g`）。中间件按同一规则核对：Nacos `2g/4Gi`（50%）、Sentinel Dashboard `1g/2Gi`（50%）、SkyWalking OAP `8g/12Gi`（67%）、Elasticsearch `16g/32Gi`（50%）均合规。
>
> ⚠️ 用 4.3.4 的脚本以 system 清单为模板生成 auth/gen/job/file 时，**必须**同步把 ConfigMap 里的 `JVM_XMS/JVM_XMX/JVM_XMN` 改为上表对应值（system 模板是 `2g/3g/1g`，直接套用到 2Gi limit 的服务会立刻 OOMKilled）。

### 4.4 Helm Chart 化

#### 4.4.1 目录结构树

```text
deploy/charts/ruoyi-cloud/
├── Chart.yaml
├── Chart.lock
├── values.yaml                     # 默认值（dev）
├── values-prod.yaml                # 生产覆盖值
├── values-canary.yaml              # 金丝雀覆盖值
├── README.md                       # Chart 使用说明
├── .helmignore
└── templates/
    ├── _helpers.tpl                # 命名与标签模板
    ├── NOTES.txt                   # 安装后提示
    ├── serviceaccount.yaml         # 每服务一个 SA（range services）
    ├── configmap.yaml              # 每服务 ConfigMap
    ├── secret.yaml                 # 仅引用外部 Secret，不生成明文
    ├── deployment.yaml             # 核心：每服务 Deployment
    ├── service.yaml                # 每服务 Service
    ├── hpa.yaml                    # 每服务 HPA（可选开关）
    ├── pdb.yaml                    # 每服务 PDB（可选开关）
    ├── ingress.yaml                # 网关 + 前端 Ingress
    ├── networkpolicy.yaml          # 命名空间网络策略
    ├── servicemonitor.yaml         # Prometheus ServiceMonitor
    ├── cronjob-backup.yaml         # 备份任务（可选）
    └── tests/
        └── test-connection.yaml    # helm test 连通性检查
```

#### 4.4.2 Chart.yaml

```yaml
apiVersion: v2
name: ruoyi-cloud
description: RuoYi-Cloud 3.6.5 企业级 Helm Chart（ACK / 三可用区高可用）
type: application
version: 3.6.5
appVersion: "3.6.5"
kubeVersion: ">=1.24.0-0"
icon: https://ruoyi.vip/images/logo.png
keywords:
  - ruoyi
  - spring-cloud
  - microservice
  - ack
home: https://git.example.com/ruoyi/ruoyi-cloud
sources:
  - https://gitee.com/y_project/RuoYi-Cloud
maintainers:
  - name: DevOps Team
    email: devops@example.com
annotations:
  category: Application
  licenses: MIT
  ruoyi.io/baseline: "v1.0"
```

#### 4.4.3 values.yaml 关键项

```yaml
# ==================== 全局 ====================
global:
  partOf: ruoyi-cloud
  managedBy: Helm
  imageRegistry: registry.cn-hangzhou.aliyuncs.com
  imageNamespace: ruoyi-prod
  imageTag: "3.6.5"
  imagePullSecrets:
    - name: ruoyi-acr-secret
  timezone: Asia/Shanghai
  # 集群内中间件地址
  nacosAddr: ruoyi-nacos.ruoyi-middleware.svc.cluster.local:8848
  nacosNamespace: ruoyi-prod
  nacosGroup: DEFAULT_GROUP
  sentinelDashboard: sentinel-dashboard.ruoyi-middleware.svc.cluster.local:8858
  skywalkingOap: skywalking-oap.ruoyi-monitoring.svc.cluster.local:11800
  existingSecret: ruoyi-runtime-secret      # 外部 Secret，Chart 不生成明文
  storageClass: alicloud-disk-essd-pl1
  uploadStorageClass: alicloud-nas-subpath
  nodePool: general
  tolerations: []

# ==================== 各服务定义 ====================
services:
  ruoyi-gateway:
    enabled: true
    component: api-gateway
    port: 8080
    replicaCount: 3
    image:
      repository: ruoyi-gateway
      tag: ""                # 空则用 global.imageTag
      pullPolicy: IfNotPresent
    resources:
      requests: { cpu: 500m, memory: 1Gi }
      limits:   { cpu: "2",  memory: 2Gi }
    jvm:
      xms: 1g
      xmx: 2g
      xmn: 512m
    hpa:
      enabled: true
      minReplicas: 3
      maxReplicas: 9
      targetCPUUtilizationPercentage: 70
      targetMemoryUtilizationPercentage: 80
    pdb:
      enabled: true
      minAvailable: 2
    probes:
      startup: { path: /actuator/health,            failureThreshold: 36, periodSeconds: 5 }
      readiness: { path: /actuator/health/readiness, failureThreshold: 3,  periodSeconds: 10 }
      liveness:  { path: /actuator/health/liveness,  failureThreshold: 4,  periodSeconds: 15 }
    initContainers:
      waitForNacos: true
      fetchAgent: true
    extraVolumes: []
    extraVolumeMounts: []
    serviceMonitor:
      enabled: true
      interval: 30s
      path: /actuator/prometheus

  ruoyi-auth:
    enabled: true
    component: auth-service
    port: 9200
    replicaCount: 3
    image: { repository: ruoyi-auth, tag: "", pullPolicy: IfNotPresent }
    resources:
      requests: { cpu: 500m, memory: 1Gi }
      limits:   { cpu: "2",  memory: 2Gi }
    jvm: { xms: 1g, xmx: 2g, xmn: 512m }
    hpa: { enabled: true, minReplicas: 3, maxReplicas: 9, targetCPUUtilizationPercentage: 70 }
    pdb: { enabled: true, minAvailable: 2 }
    extraEnv:
      - name: TOKEN_SECRET
        valueFrom:
          secretKeyRef: { name: ruoyi-runtime-secret, key: token-secret }

  ruoyi-modules-system:
    enabled: true
    component: business-service
    port: 9201
    replicaCount: 3
    image: { repository: ruoyi-modules-system, tag: "", pullPolicy: IfNotPresent }
    resources:
      requests: { cpu: 1000m, memory: 2Gi }
      limits:   { cpu: "2",   memory: 4Gi }
    jvm: { xms: 2g, xmx: 4g, xmn: 1g }
    hpa: { enabled: true, minReplicas: 3, maxReplicas: 9, targetCPUUtilizationPercentage: 70 }
    pdb: { enabled: true, minAvailable: 2 }
    upload:
      enabled: true
      claimName: ruoyi-upload-pvc
      mountPath: /data/upload

  ruoyi-modules-gen:
    enabled: true
    component: business-service
    port: 9202
    replicaCount: 2
    image: { repository: ruoyi-modules-gen, tag: "", pullPolicy: IfNotPresent }
    resources:
      requests: { cpu: 500m, memory: 1Gi }
      limits:   { cpu: "1",  memory: 2Gi }
    jvm: { xms: 1g, xmx: 2g, xmn: 512m }
    hpa: { enabled: true, minReplicas: 2, maxReplicas: 4, targetCPUUtilizationPercentage: 75 }
    pdb: { enabled: true, minAvailable: 1 }

  ruoyi-modules-job:
    enabled: true
    component: scheduler
    port: 9203
    replicaCount: 2
    image: { repository: ruoyi-modules-job, tag: "", pullPolicy: IfNotPresent }
    resources:
      requests: { cpu: 500m, memory: 1Gi }
      limits:   { cpu: "1",  memory: 2Gi }
    jvm: { xms: 1g, xmx: 2g, xmn: 512m }
    hpa: { enabled: false }          # 定时任务扩缩容会引发实例竞争，生产关闭
    pdb: { enabled: true, minAvailable: 1 }
    extraEnv:
      - name: QUARTZ_IS_CLUSTERED
        value: "true"

  ruoyi-modules-file:
    enabled: true
    component: file-service
    port: 9300
    replicaCount: 2
    image: { repository: ruoyi-modules-file, tag: "", pullPolicy: IfNotPresent }
    resources:
      requests: { cpu: 500m, memory: 1Gi }
      limits:   { cpu: "1",  memory: 2Gi }
    jvm: { xms: 1g, xmx: 2g, xmn: 512m }
    hpa: { enabled: true, minReplicas: 2, maxReplicas: 4, targetCPUUtilizationPercentage: 75 }
    pdb: { enabled: true, minAvailable: 1 }
    upload:
      enabled: true
      claimName: ruoyi-upload-pvc
      mountPath: /data/upload

# ==================== 前端 ====================
nginx:
  enabled: true
  component: frontend
  port: 80
  replicaCount: 3
  image: { repository: ruoyi-nginx, tag: "", pullPolicy: IfNotPresent }
  resources:
    requests: { cpu: 200m, memory: 256Mi }
    limits:   { cpu: "1",  memory: 1Gi }
  hpa: { enabled: true, minReplicas: 3, maxReplicas: 9, targetCPUUtilizationPercentage: 70 }
  pdb: { enabled: true, minAvailable: 2 }

# ==================== Ingress ====================
ingress:
  enabled: true
  className: nginx
  annotations:
    nginx.ingress.kubernetes.io/proxy-body-size: "200m"
    nginx.ingress.kubernetes.io/proxy-read-timeout: "600"
    nginx.ingress.kubernetes.io/proxy-send-timeout: "600"
    cert-manager.io/cluster-issuer: letsencrypt-prod
    nginx.ingress.kubernetes.io/ssl-redirect: "true"
    nginx.ingress.kubernetes.io/limit-rps: "200"
  frontend:
    host: ruoyi.example.com
    tlsSecretName: ruoyi-frontend-tls
  api:
    host: api.ruoyi.example.com
    tlsSecretName: ruoyi-api-tls

# ==================== 网络策略 ====================
networkPolicy:
  enabled: true

# ==================== 调度 ====================
scheduling:
  topologySpread: true
  antiAffinity: preferred
  nodeSelector:
    node-pool: general

# ==================== 探针与生命周期 ====================
lifecycle:
  preStopSleepSeconds: 20
  terminationGracePeriodSeconds: 60
```

#### 4.4.4 templates 设计要点

`templates/_helpers.tpl`：

```gotemplate
{{/* 基础名称 */}}
{{- define "ruoyi.name" -}}
{{- .name | trunc 63 | trimSuffix "-" -}}
{{- end -}}

{{/* 完整标签集（统一标签规范） */}}
{{- define "ruoyi.labels" -}}
app.kubernetes.io/name: {{ .name }}
app.kubernetes.io/instance: {{ .name }}-{{ .root.Values.global.env | default "prod" }}
app.kubernetes.io/version: {{ .svc.image.tag | default .root.Values.global.imageTag | quote }}
app.kubernetes.io/component: {{ .svc.component }}
app.kubernetes.io/part-of: {{ .root.Values.global.partOf }}
app.kubernetes.io/managed-by: {{ .root.Values.global.managedBy }}
helm.sh/chart: {{ .root.Chart.Name }}-{{ .root.Chart.Version }}
{{- end -}}

{{/* 选择器标签（不含 version，避免升级时选择器变化） */}}
{{- define "ruoyi.selectorLabels" -}}
app.kubernetes.io/name: {{ .name }}
app.kubernetes.io/instance: {{ .name }}-{{ .root.Values.global.env | default "prod" }}
{{- end -}}

{{/* 镜像地址 */}}
{{- define "ruoyi.image" -}}
{{- $reg := .root.Values.global.imageRegistry -}}
{{- $ns := .root.Values.global.imageNamespace -}}
{{- $tag := .svc.image.tag | default .root.Values.global.imageTag -}}
{{- printf "%s/%s/%s:%s" $reg $ns .svc.image.repository $tag -}}
{{- end -}}
```

`templates/deployment.yaml`（核心逻辑骨架）：

```gotemplate
{{- range $name, $svc := .Values.services }}
{{- if $svc.enabled }}
{{- $ctx := dict "name" $name "svc" $svc "root" $ }}
---
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: {{ $name }}
  namespace: {{ $.Release.Namespace }}
  labels:
    {{- include "ruoyi.labels" $ctx | nindent 4 }}
  annotations:
    kubernetes.io/change-cause: "helm upgrade {{ $.Release.Name }} rev {{ $.Release.Revision }}"
spec:
  replicas: {{ $svc.replicaCount }}
  revisionHistoryLimit: 5
  progressDeadlineSeconds: 600
  minReadySeconds: 30
  strategy:
    type: RollingUpdate
    rollingUpdate:
      maxSurge: {{ $.Values.rollout.maxSurge | default 1 }}
      maxUnavailable: {{ $.Values.rollout.maxUnavailable | default 0 }}
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      {{- include "ruoyi.selectorLabels" $ctx | nindent 6 }}
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        {{- include "ruoyi.labels" $ctx | nindent 8 }}
      annotations:
        prometheus.io/scrape: "true"
        prometheus.io/port: {{ $svc.port | quote }}
        prometheus.io/path: "/actuator/prometheus"
    spec:
      serviceAccountName: {{ $name }}
      imagePullSecrets:
        {{- toYaml $.Values.global.imagePullSecrets | nindent 8 }}
      terminationGracePeriodSeconds: {{ $.Values.lifecycle.terminationGracePeriodSeconds }}
      securityContext:
        runAsNonRoot: true
        runAsUser: 1000
        runAsGroup: 1000
        fsGroup: 1000
      {{- if $.Values.scheduling.topologySpread }}
      topologySpreadConstraints:
        - maxSkew: 1
          topologyKey: topology.kubernetes.io/zone
          whenUnsatisfiable: DoNotSchedule
          labelSelector:
            matchLabels:
              {{- include "ruoyi.selectorLabels" $ctx | nindent 14 }}
      {{- end }}
      nodeSelector:
        {{- toYaml $.Values.scheduling.nodeSelector | nindent 8 }}
      tolerations:
        {{- toYaml (default $.Values.global.tolerations $svc.tolerations) | nindent 8 }}
      containers:
        - name: {{ $name }}
          image: {{ include "ruoyi.image" $ctx }}
          imagePullPolicy: {{ $svc.image.pullPolicy }}
          ports:
            - name: http
              containerPort: {{ $svc.port }}
          envFrom:
            - configMapRef: { name: {{ $name }}-config }
          env:
            - name: NACOS_PASSWORD
              valueFrom:
                secretKeyRef: { name: {{ $.Values.global.existingSecret }}, key: nacos-password }
            {{- with $svc.extraEnv }}
            {{- toYaml . | nindent 12 }}
            {{- end }}
          resources:
            {{- toYaml $svc.resources | nindent 12 }}
          startupProbe:
            httpGet: { path: {{ $svc.probes.startup.path }}, port: http }
            initialDelaySeconds: 30
            periodSeconds: {{ $svc.probes.startup.periodSeconds }}
            failureThreshold: {{ $svc.probes.startup.failureThreshold }}
          readinessProbe:
            httpGet: { path: {{ $svc.probes.readiness.path }}, port: http }
            initialDelaySeconds: 10
            periodSeconds: {{ $svc.probes.readiness.periodSeconds }}
            failureThreshold: {{ $svc.probes.readiness.failureThreshold }}
          livenessProbe:
            httpGet: { path: {{ $svc.probes.liveness.path }}, port: http }
            initialDelaySeconds: 60
            periodSeconds: {{ $svc.probes.liveness.periodSeconds }}
            failureThreshold: {{ $svc.probes.liveness.failureThreshold }}
          lifecycle:
            preStop:
              exec:
                command: ["/bin/sh","-c","curl -s -X POST http://127.0.0.1:{{ $svc.port }}/actuator/shutdown || true; sleep {{ $.Values.lifecycle.preStopSleepSeconds }}"]
          volumeMounts:
            - name: skywalking-agent
              mountPath: /app/skywalking-agent
              readOnly: true
            - name: logs
              mountPath: /app/logs
            {{- if $svc.upload.enabled }}
            - name: upload
              mountPath: {{ $svc.upload.mountPath }}
            {{- end }}
      volumes:
        - name: skywalking-agent
          emptyDir: { sizeLimit: 200Mi }
        - name: logs
          emptyDir: { sizeLimit: 2Gi }
        {{- if $svc.upload.enabled }}
        - name: upload
          persistentVolumeClaim: { claimName: {{ $svc.upload.claimName }} }
        {{- end }}
{{- end }}
{{- end }}
```

#### 4.4.5 多环境 values-prod.yaml

```yaml
global:
  env: prod
  imageRegistry: registry.cn-hangzhou.aliyuncs.com
  imageNamespace: ruoyi-prod
  imageTag: "3.6.5"
  nacosAddr: ruoyi-nacos.ruoyi-middleware.svc.cluster.local:8848
  nacosNamespace: ruoyi-prod
  existingSecret: ruoyi-runtime-secret

rollout:
  maxSurge: 1
  maxUnavailable: 0

lifecycle:
  preStopSleepSeconds: 25
  terminationGracePeriodSeconds: 90

scheduling:
  topologySpread: true
  nodeSelector:
    node-pool: general

ingress:
  enabled: true
  className: nginx
  frontend:
    host: ruoyi.example.com
    tlsSecretName: ruoyi-frontend-tls
  api:
    host: api.ruoyi.example.com
    tlsSecretName: ruoyi-api-tls

# 生产最小副本提升
services:
  ruoyi-gateway:          { replicaCount: 3, hpa: { enabled: true, minReplicas: 3, maxReplicas: 9 } }
  ruoyi-auth:             { replicaCount: 3, hpa: { enabled: true, minReplicas: 3, maxReplicas: 9 } }
  ruoyi-modules-system:   { replicaCount: 3, hpa: { enabled: true, minReplicas: 3, maxReplicas: 9 } }
  ruoyi-modules-gen:      { replicaCount: 2, hpa: { enabled: true, minReplicas: 2, maxReplicas: 4 } }
  ruoyi-modules-job:      { replicaCount: 2, hpa: { enabled: false } }
  ruoyi-modules-file:     { replicaCount: 2, hpa: { enabled: true, minReplicas: 2, maxReplicas: 4 } }

nginx:
  replicaCount: 3
  hpa: { enabled: true, minReplicas: 3, maxReplicas: 9 }
```

> Helm 的 `--set` 与 `-f` 合并规则：`-f` 会与 `values.yaml` **深度合并**，`--set` 优先级最高。`values-prod.yaml` 中对 `services.ruoyi-gateway` 只写部分键，其余键从 `values.yaml` 继承。

#### 4.4.6 命令：lint / template / install / upgrade

```bash
cd deploy/charts

# ---- 1) 语法检查 ----
helm lint ruoyi-cloud -f ruoyi-cloud/values-prod.yaml
# 预期: 1 chart(s) linted, 0 chart(s) failed

# ---- 2) 渲染校验（不安装，先看 YAML）----
helm template ruoyi-prod ruoyi-cloud \
  -n ruoyi-prod \
  -f ruoyi-cloud/values-prod.yaml \
  --set global.imageTag=3.6.5 \
  > /tmp/ruoyi-rendered.yaml
kubectl apply --dry-run=server -f /tmp/ruoyi-rendered.yaml
# 预期: 全部 "configured (server dry run)"，无 Error

# ---- 3) 差异对比（升级前必做）----
helm diff upgrade ruoyi-prod ruoyi-cloud -n ruoyi-prod -f ruoyi-cloud/values-prod.yaml
# 需先安装插件: helm plugin install https://github.com/databus23/helm-diff

# ---- 4) 安装（首次）----
helm install ruoyi-prod ruoyi-cloud \
  -n ruoyi-prod --create-namespace \
  -f ruoyi-cloud/values-prod.yaml \
  --wait --timeout 15m \
  --atomic

# ---- 5) 升级 ----
helm upgrade ruoyi-prod ruoyi-cloud \
  -n ruoyi-prod \
  -f ruoyi-cloud/values-prod.yaml \
  --set global.imageTag=3.6.6 \
  --wait --timeout 15m \
  --atomic \
  --cleanup-on-fail

# ---- 6) 状态与历史 ----
helm status ruoyi-prod -n ruoyi-prod
helm history ruoyi-prod -n ruoyi-prod

# ---- 7) 回滚 ----
helm rollback ruoyi-prod 3 -n ruoyi-prod --wait --timeout 15m

# ---- 8) 卸载（慎用，PVC 不会删）----
helm uninstall ruoyi-prod -n ruoyi-prod
```

**Release 命名与版本管理规范**：

| 项 | 规范 | 示例 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Release 名 | `<项目>-<环境>` | `ruoyi-prod`、`ruoyi-canary` |
| Namespace | 与 Release 环境对应 | `ruoyi-prod` |
| Chart 版本 | 与应用版本同步 | `3.6.5` |
| appVersion | 应用版本 | `3.6.5` |
| 镜像 tag | 语义化或 Git SHA | `3.6.5`、`3.6.5-a1b2c3d` |
| 升级注释 | `--description` 必填变更单号 | `--description "CHG-2025-0118 修复登录超时"` |
| 保留历史 | 10 条 | 默认即可 |

```bash
helm upgrade ruoyi-prod ruoyi-cloud -n ruoyi-prod -f ruoyi-cloud/values-prod.yaml \
  --description "CHG-2025-0118: 升级 system 服务到 3.6.6" --atomic
```

> **【学习值】部署清单与 Helm（学习环境）**
> - 4.3 的 Deployment 清单**照抄结构，只改三处**：`replicas: 1`（生产为 gateway 3 / system 3 / gen/job/file 2）；`resources` 换成 0.8.1 表里的学习值（6 个业务微服务 `250m/512Mi → 1/1Gi`，system `1/1Gi → 1/2Gi`）；ConfigMap 的 `JVM_XMS/JVM_XMX/JVM_XMN` 换成学习值（1Gi limit → `512m/768m/256m`，2Gi limit → `1g/1536m/512m`）——**务必同步改，直接套用 system 模板会立刻 OOMKilled**。
> - **HPA 与 PDB 清单保留但先不启用**（`hpa.enabled: false`、`pdb.enabled: false`），或改用最小可用版本（`minReplicas: 1` / `maxReplicas: 2`、`minAvailable: 1`）；学习目的是读懂它们解决什么问题，不是压测。
> - **4.4 的 Helm 打包步骤必须走一遍**：`helm template` 看渲染结果 → `helm install` → 改一个 values 参数 `helm upgrade` → 故意制造一次失败发布并 `helm rollback`，理解 values 覆盖顺序与 release 历史。
> - 4.1 的镜像构建与推送照做（ACR 个人版即可），4.5 的部署顺序铁律与 4.7 的滚动更新观察照做——单副本下会看到短暂 502，正好印证 0.8.6 第 1 条。

### 4.5 按序部署操作步骤

> **部署顺序铁律**：命名空间 → Secret/SA → Nacos → MySQL/Redis（已完成）→ auth/system/gen/job/file → gateway → nginx 前端 → Ingress。
> 每步都给出：① 执行命令 ② 观察命令 ③ 就绪判据 ④ 失败中止与回退动作。

#### 4.5.1 步骤 1：命名空间与基础资源

```bash
kubectl apply -f deploy/k8s/00-namespaces.yaml
kubectl apply -f deploy/k8s/01-quota-limit.yaml
kubectl apply -f deploy/k8s/02-serviceaccounts.yaml
kubectl apply -f deploy/k8s/03-storageclass.yaml

# 观察
kubectl get ns ruoyi-prod ruoyi-middleware ruoyi-monitoring ruoyi-logging ruoyi-ingress
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod get sa,quota,limitrange
```

- **就绪判据**：5 个 ns `Active`；`ruoyi-prod` 中 `ruoyi-app`、`ruoyi-nginx` 存在；quota/limitrange 存在。
- **失败中止**：任一 apply 报错 → 修复后重试，**不进入下一步**。回退：`kubectl delete ns ruoyi-prod`（空 ns，安全）。

#### 4.5.2 步骤 2：创建 Secret

```bash
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod create secret generic ruoyi-runtime-secret \
  --from-literal=nacos-password='<NACOS_PASSWORD>' \
  --from-literal=redis-password='<REDIS_PASSWORD>' \
  --from-literal=rds-password='<RDS_PASSWORD>' \
  --from-literal=token-secret="$(openssl rand -hex 32)" \
  --dry-run=client -o yaml | kubectl apply -f -

kubectl -n ruoyi-prod get secret ruoyi-runtime-secret
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod get secret ruoyi-runtime-secret -o jsonpath='{.data}' | jq 'keys'
```

- **就绪判据**：`DATA` 列显示 4；keys 含 4 个键。
- **失败中止**：`error: failed to create secret ... already exists` → 加 `--dry-run=client -o yaml | kubectl apply -f -` 幂等处理。
- **回退**：`kubectl -n ruoyi-prod delete secret ruoyi-runtime-secret`（注意：会导致运行中 Pod 重启后无法启动，慎用）。

#### 4.5.3 步骤 3：Nacos 就绪确认（依赖步骤）

```bash
kubectl -n ruoyi-middleware get pods -l app.kubernetes.io/name=ruoyi-nacos
kubectl -n ruoyi-middleware exec ruoyi-nacos-0 -- \
  curl -s "http://127.0.0.1:8848/nacos/v1/ns/operator/servers" | jq '.servers|length'
```

- **就绪判据**：3 个 Pod 1/1 Running；servers 长度 = 3。
- **失败中止**：见 3.1.6 失败判据表；未修复不得部署业务服务。

#### 4.5.4 步骤 4：部署 auth → system → gen → job → file（无依赖顺序，但建议按此序）

```bash
# ---- auth ----
kubectl apply -f deploy/k8s/21a-auth.yaml
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod rollout status deploy/ruoyi-auth --timeout=600s
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod get pods -l app.kubernetes.io/name=ruoyi-auth -o wide

# 观察注册
kubectl -n ruoyi-middleware exec ruoyi-nacos-0 -- \
  curl -s "http://127.0.0.1:8848/nacos/v1/ns/service/list?pageNo=1&pageSize=100&namespaceId=ruoyi-prod&username=nacos&password=<NACOS_PASSWORD>" | jq '.doms'
# 预期: 出现 "ruoyi-auth"

# ---- system ----
kubectl apply -f deploy/k8s/21-system.yaml
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod rollout status deploy/ruoyi-modules-system --timeout=600s
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod exec deploy/ruoyi-modules-system -- curl -fsS http://127.0.0.1:9201/actuator/health | jq
# 预期: {"status":"UP",...}

# ---- gen / job / file ----
kubectl apply -f deploy/k8s/22-gen.yaml  deploy/k8s/23-job.yaml  deploy/k8s/24-file.yaml
for d in ruoyi-modules-gen ruoyi-modules-job ruoyi-modules-file; do
  kubectl -n ruoyi-prod rollout status deploy/$d --timeout=600s || { echo "FAILED: $d"; exit 1; }
done
```

- **就绪判据**：每个 Deployment `successfully rolled out`；Pod 数 = 副本数；Nacos 服务列表出现对应服务名；`/actuator/health` 返回 `UP`。
- **失败中止**：
  - `rollout status` 超时 → `kubectl -n ruoyi-prod describe pod <pod>` + `logs --previous`，定位后修复；修复方案不明确时**回退本服务**：
    ```bash
    kubectl -n ruoyi-prod rollout undo deploy/ruoyi-modules-system
    # 或首次部署失败时删除
    kubectl -n ruoyi-prod delete -f deploy/k8s/21-system.yaml
    ```
  - 若配置问题 → 修改 Nacos 配置并触发刷新：`curl -X POST "http://<nacos>/nacos/v1/cs/configs?...` 或控制台重新发布。

#### 4.5.5 步骤 5：部署 gateway

```bash
kubectl apply -f deploy/k8s/20-gateway.yaml
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod rollout status deploy/ruoyi-gateway --timeout=600s

# 观察路由与后端发现
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod port-forward svc/ruoyi-gateway 18080:8080 &
sleep 5
curl -s http://127.0.0.1:18080/actuator/health | jq
curl -s http://127.0.0.1:18080/actuator/gateway/routes | jq '[.[]|{route_id,uri,predicate}]'
# 预期: /actuator/health -> UP；routes 含 ruoyi-auth/ruoyi-system/ruoyi-gen/ruoyi-job/ruoyi-file
kill %1
```

- **就绪判据**：3 个 Pod Ready；health `UP`；路由表包含 5 条业务路由（或与 RuoYi 配置一致）；`lb://` 能解析到后端实例。
- **失败中止**：路由缺失 → 检查 Nacos 中 `ruoyi-gateway-routes-prod.yml` 是否发布；后端 `lb://` 解析失败 → 确认对应服务已注册且 namespace/group 一致。
- **回退**：`kubectl -n ruoyi-prod rollout undo deploy/ruoyi-gateway`。

#### 4.5.6 步骤 6：前端

```bash
kubectl apply -f deploy/k8s/30-nginx.yaml
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod rollout status deploy/ruoyi-nginx --timeout=600s
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod get pods -l app.kubernetes.io/name=ruoyi-nginx -o wide
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod exec deploy/ruoyi-nginx -- nginx -t
# 预期: syntax is ok / test is successful
```

- **就绪判据**：3 个 Pod Ready；`nginx -t` 通过；curl 本地返回 index.html。
- **回退**：`kubectl -n ruoyi-prod rollout undo deploy/ruoyi-nginx`。

#### 4.5.7 步骤 7：Ingress

```bash
kubectl apply -f deploy/k8s/31-ingress.yaml
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod get ingress
kubectl -n ruoyi-ingress logs -l app.kubernetes.io/name=ingress-nginx --tail=50 | grep -i -E 'error|ruoyi'
```

- **就绪判据**：Ingress 的 `ADDRESS` 有 IP；证书 Secret 已生成（`kubectl -n ruoyi-prod get secret | grep tls`）。
- **失败中止**：`ADDRESS` 空 → IngressClass 不匹配；证书未签发 → `kubectl -n ruoyi-prod describe certificate`。
- **回退**：`kubectl -n ruoyi-prod delete -f deploy/k8s/31-ingress.yaml`。

#### 4.5.8 部署总览与一键脚本

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# deploy-all.sh —— 按序部署，任一步失败即中止
set -euo pipefail
NS=ruoyi-prod
log() { echo "[$(date +%T)] $*"; }

log "1/7 namespaces & base"
kubectl apply -f deploy/k8s/00-namespaces.yaml
kubectl apply -f deploy/k8s/01-quota-limit.yaml
kubectl apply -f deploy/k8s/02-serviceaccounts.yaml
kubectl apply -f deploy/k8s/03-storageclass.yaml

log "2/7 secret"
kubectl -n $NS get secret ruoyi-runtime-secret >/dev/null 2>&1 || {
  echo "ERROR: ruoyi-runtime-secret 不存在，请按 4.5.2 创建"; exit 1; }

log "3/7 nacos ready check"
N=$(kubectl -n ruoyi-middleware get pods -l app.kubernetes.io/name=ruoyi-nacos \
     --field-selector=status.phase=Running --no-headers | wc -l)
[ "$N" -eq 3 ] || { echo "ERROR: Nacos 就绪节点 $N/3"; exit 1; }

log "4/7 business services"
for f in 21a-auth 21-system 22-gen 23-job 24-file; do
  kubectl apply -f deploy/k8s/$f.yaml
done
for d in ruoyi-auth ruoyi-modules-system ruoyi-modules-gen ruoyi-modules-job ruoyi-modules-file; do
  kubectl -n $NS rollout status deploy/$d --timeout=600s
done

log "5/7 gateway"
kubectl apply -f deploy/k8s/20-gateway.yaml
kubectl -n $NS rollout status deploy/ruoyi-gateway --timeout=600s

log "6/7 frontend"
kubectl apply -f deploy/k8s/30-nginx.yaml
kubectl -n $NS rollout status deploy/ruoyi-nginx --timeout=600s

log "7/7 ingress"
kubectl apply -f deploy/k8s/31-ingress.yaml

log "ALL DONE"
kubectl -n $NS get pods -o wide
```

### 4.6 部署验证

#### 4.6.1 Pod / 日志 / 事件检查

```bash
# 1) Pod 状态一览（应无 CrashLoopBackOff/ImagePullBackOff/Pending）
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod get pods -o wide
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod get pods --field-selector=status.phase!=Running
# 预期: 第二个命令输出为空（Completed 的 Job 除外）

# 2) 重启次数（生产要求近 1 小时内为 0）
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod get pods -o custom-columns=\
'NAME:.metadata.name,RESTARTS:.status.containerStatuses[*].restartCount,STATUS:.status.phase'

# 3) 非正常事件
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod get events --sort-by=.lastTimestamp | tail -30
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod get events --field-selector type=Warning | tail -20
# 预期: 无 Warning（或仅有历史已解决事件）

# 4) 关键日志关键字
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod logs -l app.kubernetes.io/name=ruoyi-gateway --tail=200 | grep -i -E 'error|exception|fail' || echo "no error in gateway"
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod logs -l app.kubernetes.io/name=ruoyi-modules-system --tail=200 | grep -i -E 'error|exception' || echo "no error in system"
# 预期: 输出 "no error ..." 或仅有可解释的 WARN

# 5) 就绪/存活探针失败检查
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod describe pod -l app.kubernetes.io/name=ruoyi-gateway | grep -A3 -i 'unhealthy\|probe'
```

失败判据：出现 `OOMKilled` → 调整 JVM_XMX 与 limits；`Liveness probe failed` → 检查 `/actuator/health/liveness` 端点是否暴露（需 `management.endpoint.health.probes.enabled=true`）。

#### 4.6.2 Nacos 服务列表核对

```bash
NACOS=ruoyi-nacos.ruoyi-middleware.svc.cluster.local:8848
curl -s "http://$NACOS/nacos/v1/ns/service/list?pageNo=1&pageSize=100&namespaceId=ruoyi-prod&username=nacos&password=<NACOS_PASSWORD>" \
  | jq -r '.doms[]' | sort
```

预期输出（6 或 7 个）：

```text
ruoyi-auth
ruoyi-gateway
ruoyi-modules-file
ruoyi-modules-gen
ruoyi-modules-job
ruoyi-modules-system
ruoyi-visual-monitor      # 可选
```

实例数核对：

```bash
for s in ruoyi-gateway ruoyi-auth ruoyi-modules-system ruoyi-modules-gen ruoyi-modules-job ruoyi-modules-file; do
  N=$(curl -s "http://$NACOS/nacos/v1/ns/instance/list?serviceName=$s&namespaceId=ruoyi-prod&username=nacos&password=<NACOS_PASSWORD>" | jq '.hosts|length')
  printf "%-26s %s instances\n" "$s" "$N"
done
# 预期: gateway 3, auth 3, system 3, gen 2, job 2, file 2
```

失败判据：实例数少于副本数 → 该服务未注册成功（检查 Nacos namespace/group 与 `spring.application.name`）。

#### 4.6.3 健康检查端点

| 服务 | 端点 | 预期 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 全部 | `/actuator/health` | `{"status":"UP"}` |
| 全部 | `/actuator/health/liveness` | `{"status":"UP"}` |
| 全部 | `/actuator/health/readiness` | `{"status":"UP"}` |
| 全部 | `/actuator/prometheus` | 输出 metrics 文本（含 `jvm_memory_used_bytes`） |
| 网关 | `/actuator/gateway/routes` | 路由 JSON 数组 |
| RuoYi 业务 | `/system/user/list`（需认证） | 无 Token 返回 401 |

```bash
for p in gateway:8080 auth:9200 modules-system:9201 modules-gen:9202 modules-job:9203 modules-file:9300; do
  S=ruoyi-$(echo $p | cut -d: -f1)
  PORT=$(echo $p | cut -d: -f2)
  echo -n "$S: "
  kubectl -n ruoyi-prod exec deploy/$S -- curl -fsS -m 5 "http://127.0.0.1:$PORT/actuator/health" 2>/dev/null | jq -r '.status' || echo "FAIL"
done
# 预期: 每行均为 UP
```

#### 4.6.4 网关路由表核对

```bash
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod exec deploy/ruoyi-gateway -- \
  curl -s http://127.0.0.1:8080/actuator/gateway/routes | jq '[.[]|{id:.route_id, uri:.uri, pred:.predicate}]'
```

预期：至少包含 5 条业务路由，`uri` 为 `lb://<服务名>`。
失败判据：`[]` → Nacos 路由配置未加载；`uri` 为 `http://` 固定地址 → 配置被本地覆盖，需改回 `lb://`。

#### 4.6.5 业务冒烟用例表

> 前置：准备 `BASE=https://api.ruoyi.example.com`（或临时 `kubectl port-forward svc/ruoyi-gateway 18080:8080`）。
> 默认账号：`admin` / 密码为部署时初始化设定（**上线前必须改密**）。

| # | 用例 | 请求样例 | 预期结果 | 通过 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | 获取验证码 | `GET /code`（`{BASE}/code`） | 200，返回 `{"code":200,"captchaEnabled":true,"uuid":"..."}` | ☐ |
| 2 | 登录 | `POST /auth/login` `{"username":"admin","password":"xxx","code":"1234","uuid":"..."}` | 200，返回 `{"code":200,"token":"eyJ..."}` | ☐ |
| 3 | 获取用户信息 | `GET /system/user/getInfo` `Authorization: Bearer <token>` | 200，含 `user`、`roles`、`permissions` | ☐ |
| 4 | 菜单路由 | `GET /system/menu/getRouters` | 200，返回菜单树 JSON 数组 | ☐ |
| 5 | 用户列表 | `GET /system/user/list?pageNum=1&pageSize=10` | 200，`total>=1`，`rows[0].userName=="admin"` | ☐ |
| 6 | 用户新增 | `POST /system/user` 带 `userName=smoketest` | 200 `{"code":200,"msg":"操作成功"}` | ☐ |
| 7 | 用户修改 | `PUT /system/user` 改 `nickName` | 200 | ☐ |
| 8 | 用户删除 | `DELETE /system/user/{ids}` | 200；再次查询列表该用户消失 | ☐ |
| 9 | 字典查询 | `GET /system/dict/data/type/sys_normal_disable` | 200，返回正常/停用两项 | ☐ |
| 10 | 参数配置 | `GET /system/config/list` | 200 | ☐ |
| 11 | 代码生成列表 | `GET /code/gen/list?pageNum=1&pageSize=10` | 200（需 gen 服务） | ☐ |
| 12 | 代码生成预览 | `GET /code/gen/preview/{tableId}` | 200，返回代码文本 | ☐ |
| 13 | 定时任务列表 | `GET /schedule/job/list?pageNum=1&pageSize=10` | 200（需 job 服务） | ☐ |
| 14 | 定时任务执行一次 | `PUT /schedule/job/run` `{"jobId":100,"jobGroup":"DEFAULT"}` | 200；日志出现任务执行记录 | ☐ |
| 15 | 文件上传 | `POST /file/upload` form-data `file=@test.png` | 200，返回 `{"code":200,"fileName":"...","url":"https://api.ruoyi.example.com/statics/..."}` | ☐ |
| 16 | 文件下载 | `GET <返回的url>` | 200，文件内容与上传一致（md5 相同） | ☐ |
| 17 | 未认证拦截 | `GET /system/user/list`（无 Token） | 401，`{"code":401,"msg":"认证失败..."}` | ☐ |
| 18 | 越权拦截 | 用无权限账号访问管理接口 | 403，提示无权限 | ☐ |
| 19 | 网关限流 | 快速发送 300 次 `GET /code` | 出现 429 或按规则限流，且服务不崩 | ☐ |
| 20 | 熔断降级 | 停止 system 服务后调用其接口 | 返回降级响应（非 500 堆栈） | ☐ |

完整 curl 冒烟脚本：

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# smoke-test.sh —— 若依生产冒烟测试
set -uo pipefail
BASE="${BASE:-https://api.ruoyi.example.com}"
USER_NAME="${SMOKE_USER:-admin}"
PASS="${SMOKE_PASS:?请设置 SMOKE_PASS}"
PASS_CNT=0; FAIL_CNT=0
chk() { # chk <name> <expected_substr> <actual>
  if echo "$3" | grep -q "$2"; then echo "PASS  $1"; PASS_CNT=$((PASS_CNT+1));
  else echo "FAIL  $1  (expect: $2)"; echo "      actual: ${3:0:200}"; FAIL_CNT=$((FAIL_CNT+1)); fi
}

# 1) 验证码
CAP=$(curl -s -m 10 "$BASE/code")
UUID=$(echo "$CAP" | jq -r '.uuid // empty')
chk "captcha" '"code":200' "$CAP"

# 2) 登录
LOGIN=$(curl -s -m 10 -X POST "$BASE/auth/login" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -d "{\"username\":\"$USER_NAME\",\"password\":\"$PASS\",\"code\":\"1234\",\"uuid\":\"$UUID\"}")
TOKEN=$(echo "$LOGIN" | jq -r '.token // empty')
chk "login" '"code":200' "$LOGIN"
[ -n "$TOKEN" ] || { echo "FATAL: no token, abort"; exit 1; }
AUTH="Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN"

# 3) 用户信息
chk "getInfo" 'admin' "$(curl -s -m 10 -H "$AUTH" "$BASE/system/user/getInfo")"
# 4) 菜单
chk "getRouters" 'component' "$(curl -s -m 10 -H "$AUTH" "$BASE/system/menu/getRouters")"
# 5) 用户列表
chk "userList" '"total"' "$(curl -s -m 10 -H "$AUTH" "$BASE/system/user/list?pageNum=1&pageSize=10")"
# 6) 字典
chk "dictData" 'sys_normal_disable' "$(curl -s -m 10 -H "$AUTH" "$BASE/system/dict/data/type/sys_normal_disable")"
# 7) 代码生成
chk "genList" '"code":200' "$(curl -s -m 10 -H "$AUTH" "$BASE/code/gen/list?pageNum=1&pageSize=10")"
# 8) 定时任务
chk "jobList" '"code":200' "$(curl -s -m 10 -H "$AUTH" "$BASE/schedule/job/list?pageNum=1&pageSize=10")"
# 9) 未认证
chk "unauth401" '401' "$(curl -s -m 10 -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' "$BASE/system/user/list")"

echo "----------------------------------------"
echo "smoke result: PASS=$PASS_CNT FAIL=$FAIL_CNT"
[ "$FAIL_CNT" -eq 0 ] || exit 1
```

### 4.7 滚动更新、灰度与回滚

#### 4.7.1 滚动更新参数建议

| 服务 | maxSurge | maxUnavailable | minReadySeconds | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| gateway | 1 | 0 | 30 | 零不可用，先扩后缩 |
| auth | 1 | 0 | 30 | 登录不可中断 |
| modules-system | 1 | 0 | 30 | 核心业务 |
| modules-gen | 1 | 1 | 15 | 低频，允许短暂降容 |
| modules-job | 1 | 0 | 30 | Quartz 集群，避免任务漂移 |
| modules-file | 1 | 1 | 15 | 上传短暂重试可接受 |
| nginx | 1 | 0 | 10 | 静态资源 |

```bash
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod patch deploy ruoyi-gateway -p \
  '{"spec":{"strategy":{"rollingUpdate":{"maxSurge":1,"maxUnavailable":0}}}}'
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod patch deploy ruoyi-modules-gen -p \
  '{"spec":{"strategy":{"rollingUpdate":{"maxSurge":1,"maxUnavailable":1}},"minReadySeconds":15}}'
```

#### 4.7.2 滚动更新操作与观察

```bash
# 更新镜像
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod set image deploy/ruoyi-modules-system \
  ruoyi-modules-system=<YOUR_ACR_REGISTRY>/ruoyi-prod/ruoyi-modules-system:3.6.6

# 观察滚动过程（另开一个终端）
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod get pods -l app.kubernetes.io/name=ruoyi-modules-system -w

# 等待完成
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod rollout status deploy/ruoyi-modules-system --timeout=600s
# 预期: deployment "ruoyi-modules-system" successfully rolled out

# 记录变更原因
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod annotate deploy/ruoyi-modules-system \
  kubernetes.io/change-cause="CHG-2025-0118 升级至 3.6.6" --overwrite

# 观察期间的关键指标（另一终端）
watch -n2 "kubectl -n ruoyi-prod get pods -l app.kubernetes.io/name=ruoyi-modules-system; \
  kubectl -n ruoyi-prod get events --sort-by=.lastTimestamp | tail -5"
```

**就绪判据**：滚动期间可用 Pod ≥ 3（maxUnavailable=0）；新 Pod readinessProbe 通过后旧 Pod 才终止；业务接口无 5xx 增长。
**失败中止与回退**：

```bash
# 滚动卡住 → 查看原因
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod describe deploy/ruoyi-modules-system | tail -20
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod rollout status deploy/ruoyi-modules-system --watch=false

# 立即回退
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod rollout undo deploy/ruoyi-modules-system
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod rollout status deploy/ruoyi-modules-system --timeout=600s
# 预期: rolled back
```

#### 4.7.3 金丝雀发布方案

**方案 A：副本比例金丝雀（简单，推荐起步）**

```bash
# 1) 部署 canary 版本（独立 Deployment，2 个副本，带 version=canary 标签）
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod create -f - <<'EOF'
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: ruoyi-modules-system-canary
  namespace: ruoyi-prod
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-modules-system-canary
    app.kubernetes.io/instance: ruoyi-modules-system-canary
    app.kubernetes.io/version: "3.6.6"
    app.kubernetes.io/component: business-service
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
    app.kubernetes.io/managed-by: Helm
spec:
  replicas: 1
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-modules-system
      app.kubernetes.io/version: "3.6.6"
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-modules-system     # 与稳定版同一 Service 选择器
        app.kubernetes.io/version: "3.6.6"
        app.kubernetes.io/instance: ruoyi-modules-system-prod
        app.kubernetes.io/component: business-service
        app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
        app.kubernetes.io/managed-by: Helm
    spec:
      containers:
        - name: ruoyi-modules-system
          image: <YOUR_ACR_REGISTRY>/ruoyi-prod/ruoyi-modules-system:3.6.6
          ports:
            - name: http
              containerPort: 9201
          envFrom:
            - configMapRef:
                name: ruoyi-modules-system-config
          resources:
            requests: { cpu: 1000m, memory: 2Gi }
            limits: { cpu: "2", memory: 4Gi }
EOF

# 2) 观察 30 分钟：错误率、P99、日志异常
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod logs -l app.kubernetes.io/version=3.6.6 --tail=200 | grep -i error || echo "canary clean"
# Grafana 查询：sum(rate(http_server_requests_seconds_count{status=~"5..",version="3.6.6"}[5m]))
# 预期: 5xx 速率 ≈ 0

# 3) 通过 → 全量升级；不通过 → 删除 canary
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod delete deploy ruoyi-modules-system-canary
```

**方案 B：Nacos metadata 版本路由（精细，需网关支持）**

```yaml
# 网关增加基于 metadata.version 的灰度路由
spring:
  cloud:
    gateway:
      routes:
        - id: ruoyi-system-canary
          uri: lb://ruoyi-modules-system
          predicates:
            - Path=/system/**
            - Header=X-Gray, canary          # 或按用户 ID/权重
          filters:
            - StripPrefix=1
        - id: ruoyi-system
          uri: lb://ruoyi-modules-system
          predicates:
            - Path=/system/**
          filters:
            - StripPrefix=1
```

需配合 Spring Cloud LoadBalancer 自定义 `ServiceInstanceListSupplier` 按 `metadata.version` 过滤（RuoYi 需小幅改造，改造清单记入 4.2.3）。

**方案 C：Ingress 金丝雀注解**（适用于前端/整体流量切分，见 5.3.5）

#### 4.7.4 版本回滚演练步骤与判定

```bash
# ---- 演练目标：5 分钟内完成一次可控回滚 ----

# 1) 记录当前状态
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod get deploy ruoyi-modules-system -o jsonpath='{.spec.template.spec.containers[0].image}{"\n"}'
# 预期: ...ruoyi-modules-system:3.6.5
helm history ruoyi-prod -n ruoyi-prod | tail -5

# 2) 制造一次"坏发布"（用错误镜像模拟）
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod set image deploy/ruoyi-modules-system \
  ruoyi-modules-system=<YOUR_ACR_REGISTRY>/ruoyi-prod/ruoyi-modules-system:3.6.6-bad
# 观察：新 Pod ImagePullBackOff 或 CrashLoop，旧 Pod 因 maxUnavailable=0 仍在服务

# 3) 判定是否需回滚（满足任一即回滚）
#    a) 新 Pod 60s 内未 Ready
#    b) 5xx 比例 > 1%
#    c) P99 延迟较基线上升 > 50%
#    d) 关键业务用例失败
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod get pods -l app.kubernetes.io/name=ruoyi-modules-system

# 4) 回滚
TIME_START=$(date +%s)
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod rollout undo deploy/ruoyi-modules-system
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod rollout status deploy/ruoyi-modules-system --timeout=600s
TIME_END=$(date +%s)
echo "rollback took $((TIME_END-TIME_START)) seconds"

# 5) 验证回滚后状态
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod get deploy ruoyi-modules-system -o jsonpath='{.spec.template.spec.containers[0].image}{"\n"}'
# 预期: 回到 3.6.5
curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}\n' "https://api.ruoyi.example.com/system/user/list"
# 预期: 401（服务可用）
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod get rs -l app.kubernetes.io/name=ruoyi-modules-system
# 预期: 旧 RS 的 DESIRED 恢复为 3，新 RS 缩为 0

# 6) 记录演练结果
cat >> rollback-drill.log <<EOF
$(date -Is) 演练: system 服务回滚 | 耗时 $((TIME_END-TIME_START))s | 结果: 成功
EOF
```

**通过标准**：回滚耗时 ≤ 300 秒；回滚后所有 Pod Ready；核心接口恢复可用；无数据不一致。
**判定失败**：`rollout undo` 报 `no rollout history found` → 该 Deployment 无历史版本（首次部署），此时只能 `kubectl apply` 回旧清单或 `helm rollback`。
**Helm 场景回滚**：

```bash
helm rollback ruoyi-prod 3 -n ruoyi-prod --wait --timeout 15m
helm status ruoyi-prod -n ruoyi-prod | grep -E 'STATUS|REVISION'
# 预期: STATUS: deployed
```

---

## 5. 前端接入与 Ingress 配置

### 5.1 前端镜像构建

#### 5.1.1 多阶段 Dockerfile（前端）

`deploy/docker/Dockerfile.ui`：

```dockerfile
# syntax=docker/dockerfile:1.6
# ==================== Stage 1: 构建 ====================
FROM registry.cn-hangzhou.aliyuncs.com/ruoyi-prod/node:18.20.4-alpine AS builder
WORKDIR /app

# 使用国内 npm 镜像加速
RUN npm config set registry https://registry.npmmirror.com && \
    npm config set fetch-timeout 60000

# 先复制依赖清单，利用层缓存
COPY package.json package-lock.json ./
RUN --mount=type=cache,target=/root/.npm npm ci --no-audit --no-fund

# 复制源码并构建
COPY . .
# 生产环境变量（VUE_APP_BASE_API 采用相对路径 /prod-api）
ENV NODE_OPTIONS=--max-old-space-size=4096
RUN npm run build:prod

# ==================== Stage 2: 运行 ====================
FROM registry.cn-hangzhou.aliyuncs.com/ruoyi-prod/nginx:1.24-alpine
LABEL org.opencontainers.image.title="ruoyi-nginx" \
      org.opencontainers.image.version="3.6.5" \
      org.opencontainers.image.vendor="ruoyi-prod"

ENV TZ=Asia/Shanghai
RUN apk add --no-cache tzdata curl && \
    ln -snf /usr/share/zoneinfo/$TZ /etc/localtime && echo $TZ > /etc/timezone && \
    addgroup -g 1000 ruoyi && adduser -u 1000 -G ruoyi -D -s /sbin/nologin ruoyi && \
    # 允许非 root 绑定 80 端口：改为 8080，由 Service 映射；或授予 capabilities
    sed -i 's/user  nginx;/user  ruoyi;/' /etc/nginx/nginx.conf && \
    mkdir -p /var/cache/nginx /var/log/nginx /var/run && \
    chown -R 1000:1000 /var/cache/nginx /var/log/nginx /var/run /usr/share/nginx/html

# 静态产物
COPY --from=builder /app/dist /usr/share/nginx/html
# Nginx 配置（挂载式，便于运行时覆盖）
COPY deploy/docker/nginx.conf /etc/nginx/nginx.conf
COPY deploy/docker/conf.d/ /etc/nginx/conf.d/

# 健康检查
HEALTHCHECK --interval=30s --timeout=3s --start-period=10s --retries=3 \
  CMD curl -fsS http://127.0.0.1:8080/healthz || exit 1

USER 1000:1000
EXPOSE 8080
CMD ["nginx", "-g", "daemon off;"]
```

> **注意**：非 root 无法绑定 80。容器内监听 **8080**，由 Service 把 80 映射到 8080；Ingress 指向 Service 的 80。

#### 5.1.2 nginx.conf 全文（主配置）

`deploy/docker/nginx.conf`：

```nginx
user  ruoyi;
worker_processes  auto;
worker_rlimit_nofile 1048576;
pid        /var/run/nginx.pid;
error_log  /var/log/nginx/error.log warn;

events {
    use                 epoll;
    worker_connections  16384;
    multi_accept        on;
    accept_mutex        off;
}

http {
    include       /etc/nginx/mime.types;
    default_type  application/octet-stream;

    # ---------- 日志（JSON 便于 Filebeat 解析）----------
    log_format json_ruoyi escape=json
      '{"time":"$time_iso8601",'
      '"remote_addr":"$remote_addr",'
      '"x_forwarded_for":"$http_x_forwarded_for",'
      '"request_method":"$request_method",'
      '"request_uri":"$request_uri",'
      '"status":$status,'
      '"body_bytes_sent":$body_bytes_sent,'
      '"request_time":$request_time,'
      '"upstream_response_time":"$upstream_response_time",'
      '"http_referer":"$http_referer",'
      '"http_user_agent":"$http_user_agent",'
      '"trace_id":"$http_x_trace_id",'
      '"upstream_addr":"$upstream_addr"}';

    access_log  /var/log/nginx/access.log json_ruoyi;

    # ---------- 基础 ----------
    sendfile            on;
    tcp_nopush          on;
    tcp_nodelay         on;
    keepalive_timeout   75s;
    keepalive_requests  10000;
    types_hash_max_size 4096;
    server_tokens       off;
    reset_timedout_connection on;

    # ---------- 请求体与超时（大文件上传）----------
    client_max_body_size        200m;
    client_body_buffer_size     1m;
    client_body_timeout         300s;
    client_header_timeout       60s;
    send_timeout                300s;

    # ---------- 压缩 ----------
    gzip                on;
    gzip_vary           on;
    gzip_proxied        any;
    gzip_comp_level     6;
    gzip_min_length     1024;
    gzip_buffers        16 8k;
    gzip_http_version   1.1;
    gzip_types
        text/plain
        text/css
        text/xml
        text/javascript
        application/javascript
        application/x-javascript
        application/json
        application/xml
        application/xml+rss
        application/vnd.ms-fontobject
        application/wasm
        image/svg+xml
        font/ttf
        font/otf
        font/woff
        font/woff2;

    # ---------- 限流（本地兜底，主限流在 Ingress/Sentinel）----------
    limit_req_zone  $binary_remote_addr zone=ruoyi_api:20m rate=100r/s;
    limit_conn_zone $binary_remote_addr zone=ruoyi_conn:10m;

    # ---------- 上游（K8s Service DNS）----------
    upstream ruoyi-gateway_upstream {
        # K8s 下由 Service 做负载，这里只配一个后端即可
        server ruoyi-gateway.ruoyi-prod.svc.cluster.local:8080 max_fails=3 fail_timeout=10s;
        keepalive 64;
        keepalive_timeout 60s;
        keepalive_requests 1000;
    }

    # ---------- 安全响应头 ----------
    add_header X-Content-Type-Options   "nosniff"            always;
    add_header X-Frame-Options          "SAMEORIGIN"         always;
    add_header X-XSS-Protection         "1; mode=block"      always;
    add_header Referrer-Policy          "strict-origin-when-cross-origin" always;
    add_header Permissions-Policy       "geolocation=(), microphone=(), camera=()" always;
    add_header Strict-Transport-Security "max-age=31536000; includeSubDomains" always;
    # 按需收紧 CSP（先观察再启用）
    # add_header Content-Security-Policy "default-src 'self'; img-src 'self' data: https:; style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline' 'unsafe-eval'" always;

    # ---------- WebSocket/SSE 升级映射 ----------
    map $http_upgrade $connection_upgrade {
        default upgrade;
        ''      close;
    }
    map $http_x_trace_id $trace_id {
        default $http_x_trace_id;
        ''      $request_id;
    }

    include /etc/nginx/conf.d/*.conf;
}
```

#### 5.1.3 站点配置 conf.d/ruoyi.conf

```nginx
server {
    listen       8080 default_server;
    server_name  _;
    root         /usr/share/nginx/html;
    index        index.html;

    charset utf-8;

    # ---------- 健康检查（给 K8s 探针用，不打日志）----------
    location = /healthz {
        access_log off;
        add_header Content-Type text/plain;
        return 200 "ok\n";
    }

    # ---------- 静态资源长缓存（带 hash 的文件）----------
    location ~* \.(?:js|css|woff2?|ttf|otf|eot|svg|png|jpe?g|gif|webp|ico|mp4|wasm)$ {
        expires 1y;
        add_header Cache-Control "public, max-age=31536000, immutable";
        access_log off;
        try_files $uri =404;
    }

    # ---------- index.html 不缓存（保证发版即时生效）----------
    location = /index.html {
        add_header Cache-Control "no-cache, no-store, must-revalidate";
        add_header Pragma "no-cache";
        expires -1;
    }

    # ---------- API 反代到网关 ----------
    location /prod-api/ {
```
