```yaml
    op: ">"
    threshold: 500
    period: 10
    count: 2
    silence-period: 10
    message: "实例 {name} 的 GC 时间超过 500ms"
  # P99
  service_p99_rule:
    metrics-name: service_p99
    op: ">"
    threshold: 3000
    period: 10
    count: 3
    silence-period: 10
    message: "服务 {name} 的 P99 超过 3000ms"

# ---------- 告警投递（webhook 到 Alertmanager，再由 Alertmanager 分发多渠道）----------
webhooks:
  - http://ruoyi-alertmanager.ruoyi-monitoring.svc.cluster.local:9093/api/v2/alerts
  # 也可直接投递钉钉（注意钉钉要求特定 JSON 结构，建议经 Alertmanager 转换）
  # - https://oapi.dingtalk.com/robot/send?access_token=<ALERT_WEBHOOK_DINGTALK>
```

> SkyWalking 的 webhook 需要接收方兼容其 JSON 结构。**推荐做法**：SkyWalking 先投到 Alertmanager（或一个轻量转换服务 `webhook-adapter`），由 Alertmanager 统一做分级、去重、抑制与多渠道分发（见 6.5）。

#### 6.2.6 Trace 全链路验证（traceId 贯穿网关→auth→system）

```bash
# 1) 发起一次带 trace 的请求（登录 + 查询用户列表）
CAP=$(curl -s https://api.ruoyi.example.com/code); UUID=$(echo $CAP | jq -r .uuid)
TOKEN=$(curl -s -X POST https://api.ruoyi.example.com/auth/login \
  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -d "{\"username\":\"admin\",\"password\":\"<ADMIN_PASSWORD>\",\"code\":\"1234\",\"uuid\":\"$UUID\"}" | jq -r .token)
curl -s -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" \
  "https://api.ruoyi.example.com/system/user/list?pageNum=1&pageSize=10" -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}\n'
# 预期: 200

# 2) 在 SkyWalking UI 中按时间范围查询 Trace，应看到：
#    ruoyi-gateway → ruoyi-auth（登录请求）
#    ruoyi-gateway → ruoyi-modules-system（用户列表请求）
#    每个 Trace 的 span 包含同一 traceId

# 3) 用 GraphQL 查询 Trace（自动化校验）
QUERY='{"query":"query{queryBasicTraces(condition:{serviceId:\"<SERVICE_ID>\",queryDuration:{start:\"'$(date -u -d '-10 min' +%Y-%m-%dT%H%M%S)'\",end:\"'$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H%M%S)'\",step:MINUTE},traceState:ALL,queryOrder:BY_DURATION,paging:{pageNum:1,pageSize:10}}){traces{segmentId,traceIds,duration,endpointNames}}}'
curl -s -X POST http://127.0.0.1:18082/graphql -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d "$QUERY" | jq

# 4) 校验 traceId 在日志中也存在（便于日志与链路关联）
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod logs deploy/ruoyi-gateway --tail=200 | grep -oE 'tid=[a-f0-9.]+' | head -3
kubectl -n ruoyi-prod logs deploy/ruoyi-modules-system --tail=200 | grep -oE 'tid=[a-f0-9.]+' | head -3
# 预期: 找到相同 traceId 前缀的日志（需在 logback pattern 中加入 %X{tid}）
```

**日志中注入 traceId 的 logback 配置**（改 `logback-spring.xml`）：

```xml
<pattern>%d{yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss.SSS} [%thread] %-5level [%X{tid:-}] %logger{50} - %msg%n</pattern>
```

并在 SkyWalking Agent 中开启 logback MDC 插件（`apm-toolkit-logback-1.x` 已随 Agent 提供；`agent/plugins` 中含 `apm-toolkit-logback-1.x` 与 `toolkit-activation-logback-1.x` 插件，默认加载）。

**全链路通过标准**：
1. SkyWalking 服务列表出现 6 个业务服务；
2. 任一业务请求可在 UI 中查到 Trace，且**同一 Trace 跨越 ≥2 个服务**；
3. Trace 中的 span 名称包含实际接口路径（如 `/system/user/list`）；
4. 应用日志中的 `tid` 与 Trace 的 traceId 一致；
5. Kibana 中可按 traceId 检索到多个服务的日志（见 6.4.6）。

### 6.3 Prometheus + Grafana

#### 6.3.1 kube-prometheus-stack 安装

```bash
helm repo add prometheus-community https://prometheus-community.github.io/helm-charts
helm repo update

cat > /tmp/kps-values.yaml <<'EOF'
# ==================== 全局 ====================
global:
  rbac:
    create: true
  nodeSelector:
    node-pool: general

# ==================== Prometheus ====================
prometheus:
  enabled: true
  prometheusSpec:
    replicas: 2
    retention: 15d
    retentionSize: 40GB
    scrapeInterval: 30s
    evaluationInterval: 30s
    # 选择所有命名空间的 ServiceMonitor/PodMonitor
    serviceMonitorSelectorNilUsesHelmValues: false
    podMonitorSelectorNilUsesHelmValues: false
    probeSelectorNilUsesHelmValues: false
    ruleSelectorNilUsesHelmValues: false
    resources:
      requests: { cpu: 1000m, memory: 4Gi }
      limits:   { cpu: "2",   memory: 8Gi }
    storageSpec:
      volumeClaimTemplate:
        spec:
          storageClassName: alicloud-disk-essd-pl2
          accessModes: ["ReadWriteOnce"]
          resources:
            requests:
              storage: 100Gi
    nodeSelector:
      node-pool: general
    tolerations:
      - key: dedicated
        operator: Exists
        effect: NoSchedule
    # 拓扑打散：两副本跨 AZ
    topologySpreadConstraints:
      - maxSkew: 1
        topologyKey: kubernetes.io/zone
        whenUnsatisfiable: ScheduleAnyway
        labelSelector:
          matchLabels:
            app.kubernetes.io/name: prometheus
    # 外部标签，便于多集群聚合
    externalLabels:
      cluster: ack-ruoyi-prod
      region: cn-hangzhou
    # 保留 kubelet/cAdvisor 等默认抓取
    additionalScrapeConfigs: []
  service:
    type: ClusterIP

# ==================== Alertmanager ====================
alertmanager:
  enabled: true
  alertmanagerSpec:
    replicas: 2
    resources:
      requests: { cpu: 100m, memory: 256Mi }
      limits:   { cpu: 500m, memory: 512Mi }
    storage:
      volumeClaimTemplate:
        spec:
          storageClassName: alicloud-disk-essd-pl1
          accessModes: ["ReadWriteOnce"]
          resources:
            requests:
              storage: 10Gi
    nodeSelector:
      node-pool: general

# ==================== Grafana ====================
grafana:
  enabled: true
  replicas: 2
  resources:
    requests: { cpu: 100m, memory: 256Mi }
    limits:   { cpu: 500m, memory: 1Gi }
  persistence:
    enabled: true
    storageClassName: alicloud-disk-essd-pl1
    size: 20Gi
  adminPassword: "<GRAFANA_ADMIN_PASSWORD>"
  nodeSelector:
    node-pool: general
  ingress:
    enabled: false      # 统一走 5.3.1 的 ruoyi-console Ingress
  grafana.ini:
    server:
      root_url: https://grafana.example.com
    security:
      cookie_secure: true
      disable_gravatar: true
    auth.anonymous:
      enabled: false
    analytics:
      reporting_enabled: false
      check_for_updates: false
    log:
      level: warn

# ==================== node-exporter ====================
nodeExporter:
  enabled: true

prometheus-node-exporter:
  nodeSelector:
    node-pool: general
  tolerations:
    - operator: Exists

# ==================== kube-state-metrics ====================
kubeStateMetrics:
  enabled: true

kube-state-metrics:
  replicas: 2
  nodeSelector:
    node-pool: general
  tolerations:
    - operator: Exists

# ==================== 默认规则裁剪（避免噪声告警）====================
defaultRules:
  create: true
  rules:
    alertmanager: true
    etcd: false            # ACK 托管版无 etcd 指标
    configReloaders: true
    general: true
    k8s: true
    kubeApiserver: true
    kubeApiserverAvailability: true
    kubeApiserverBurnrate: false
    kubeApiserverHistogram: false
    kubeApiserverSlos: false
    kubelet: true
    kubeProxy: true
    kubePrometheusGeneral: true
    kubePrometheusNodeRecording: true
    kubernetesApps: true
    kubernetesResources: true
    kubernetesStorage: true
    kubernetesSystem: true
    network: true
    node: true
    nodeExporterAlerting: true
    nodeExporterRecording: true
    prometheus: true
EOF

kubectl create namespace ruoyi-monitoring --dry-run=client -o yaml | kubectl apply -f -

helm upgrade --install kube-prometheus-stack prometheus-community/kube-prometheus-stack \
  --namespace ruoyi-monitoring --version 58.2.2 \
  -f /tmp/kps-values.yaml \
  --wait --timeout 20m

kubectl -n ruoyi-monitoring get pods
# 预期: prometheus-kube-prometheus-stack-prometheus-0/1、alertmanager-0/1、grafana-xxx 均 Running
```

验证：

```bash
kubectl -n ruoyi-monitoring port-forward svc/kube-prometheus-stack-prometheus 19090:9090 &
curl -s http://127.0.0.1:19090/-/ready
# 预期: Prometheus Server is Ready.
curl -s 'http://127.0.0.1:19090/api/v1/targets?state=active' | jq '[.data.activeTargets[]|select(.health!="up")|{scrapeUrl,health,lastError}]'
# 预期: 空数组（或仅有已知可忽略项）
curl -s 'http://127.0.0.1:19090/api/v1/rules' | jq '[.data.groups[].rules[]|.name]|length'
# 预期: >50（默认规则已加载）
kill %1
```

失败判据：

| 现象 | 原因 | 处理 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Prometheus Pod Pending | PVC 未绑定或节点资源不足 | 查看 events，检查 StorageClass 与节点余量 |
| target down（kubelet 等） | 端口/证书问题 | ACK 托管版已适配，检查 ServiceMonitor 是否被 `selectorNilUsesHelmValues` 过滤 |
| Grafana 无法登录 | adminPassword 未生效（已存在 Secret） | 删除 `kube-prometheus-stack-grafana` Secret 后重新 upgrade |
| alertmanager 配置错误 | values 中 receivers 不合法 | `kubectl -n ruoyi-monitoring logs alertmanager-0` |

#### 6.3.2 ServiceMonitor / PodMonitor 示例

**Java 应用 JVM 指标（业务服务）**：

```yaml
apiVersion: monitoring.coreos.com/v1
kind: ServiceMonitor
metadata:
  name: ruoyi-java-services
  namespace: ruoyi-monitoring
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
    release: kube-prometheus-stack     # 必须匹配 Prometheus 的 selector
spec:
  namespaceSelector:
    matchNames:
      - ruoyi-prod
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
  endpoints:
    - port: http
      path: /actuator/prometheus
      interval: 30s
      scrapeTimeout: 10s
      honorLabels: true
      relabelings:
        - sourceLabels: [__meta_kubernetes_pod_label_app_kubernetes_io_name]
          targetLabel: service
        - sourceLabels: [__meta_kubernetes_pod_label_app_kubernetes_io_version]
          targetLabel: version
        - sourceLabels: [__meta_kubernetes_pod_name]
          targetLabel: pod
        - sourceLabels: [__meta_kubernetes_namespace]
          targetLabel: namespace
      metricRelabelings:
        # 丢弃高基数/无用指标，控制存储成本
        - sourceLabels: [__name__]
          regex: "jvm_classes_loaded_classes|jvm_classes_loaded|process_files_open_files|system_cpu_count"
          action: drop
        - sourceLabels: [__name__]
          regex: "http_server_requests_seconds_(bucket|sum)"
          action: keep     # 保留 histogram 用于 P99
```

> **注意**：`metricRelabelings` 中连续的 keep 与 drop 会互相覆盖，实际使用时**只保留一组**；上面示例用于说明语法，落地时应拆成两条规则或简化。

**有状态服务（Nacos / ES）**：

```yaml
apiVersion: monitoring.coreos.com/v1
kind: ServiceMonitor
metadata:
  name: ruoyi-nacos
  namespace: ruoyi-monitoring
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
    release: kube-prometheus-stack
spec:
  namespaceSelector:
    matchNames: [ruoyi-middleware]
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-nacos
  endpoints:
    - port: http
      path: /nacos/actuator/prometheus
      interval: 30s
      scrapeTimeout: 10s
      metricRelabelings:
        - sourceLabels: [__name__]
          regex: "nacos_monitor.*"
          action: keep
---
apiVersion: monitoring.coreos.com/v1
kind: ServiceMonitor
metadata:
  name: ruoyi-elasticsearch
  namespace: ruoyi-monitoring
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
    release: kube-prometheus-stack
spec:
  namespaceSelector:
    matchNames: [ruoyi-logging]
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-elasticsearch
  endpoints:
    - port: http
      path: /_prometheus/metrics
      interval: 30s
      scrapeTimeout: 15s
```

> ES 7.17 需安装 `prometheus-exporter` 插件，或使用独立的 `elasticsearch-exporter` Deployment。推荐后者（更稳定）：

```yaml
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: elasticsearch-exporter
  namespace: ruoyi-logging
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: elasticsearch-exporter
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
spec:
  replicas: 1
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app.kubernetes.io/name: elasticsearch-exporter
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app.kubernetes.io/name: elasticsearch-exporter
        app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
    spec:
      imagePullSecrets:
        - name: ruoyi-acr-secret
      nodeSelector:
        node-pool: logging
      tolerations:
        - key: dedicated
          operator: Equal
          value: logging
          effect: NoSchedule
      containers:
        - name: exporter
          image: <YOUR_ACR_REGISTRY>/ruoyi-prod/elasticsearch-exporter:1.7.0
          args:
            - "--es.uri=http://ruoyi-elasticsearch.ruoyi-logging.svc.cluster.local:9200"
            - "--es.all"
            - "--es.indices"
            - "--es.shards"
            - "--es.timeout=30s"
            - "--web.listen-address=:9114"
          ports:
            - name: metrics
              containerPort: 9114
          resources:
            requests: { cpu: 100m, memory: 128Mi }
            limits: { cpu: 500m, memory: 512Mi }
---
apiVersion: v1
kind: Service
metadata:
  name: elasticsearch-exporter
  namespace: ruoyi-logging
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: elasticsearch-exporter
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
spec:
  ports:
    - name: metrics
      port: 9114
      targetPort: 9114
  selector:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: elasticsearch-exporter
---
apiVersion: monitoring.coreos.com/v1
kind: ServiceMonitor
metadata:
  name: elasticsearch-exporter
  namespace: ruoyi-monitoring
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
    release: kube-prometheus-stack
spec:
  namespaceSelector:
    matchNames: [ruoyi-logging]
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app.kubernetes.io/name: elasticsearch-exporter
  endpoints:
    - port: metrics
      interval: 30s
```

**MySQL exporter（RDS）**：

```yaml
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: mysqld-exporter
  namespace: ruoyi-monitoring
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: mysqld-exporter
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
spec:
  replicas: 1
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app.kubernetes.io/name: mysqld-exporter
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app.kubernetes.io/name: mysqld-exporter
        app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
    spec:
      imagePullSecrets:
        - name: ruoyi-acr-secret
      nodeSelector:
        node-pool: general
      containers:
        - name: exporter
          image: <YOUR_ACR_REGISTRY>/ruoyi-prod/mysqld-exporter:v0.15.1
          env:
            - name: MYSQLD_EXPORTER_PASSWORD
              valueFrom:
                secretKeyRef:
                  name: ruoyi-runtime-secret
                  key: rds-password
          args:
            - "--mysqld.address=<RDS_ENDPOINT>:3306"
            - "--mysqld.username=<RDS_USER>"
            - "--collect.global_status"
            - "--collect.global_variables"
            - "--collect.engine_innodb_status"
            - "--collect.slave_status"
            - "--collect.info_schema.processlist"
            - "--no-collect.slave_status"   # RDS 主备关系由控制台展示，指标无权限
          ports:
            - name: metrics
              containerPort: 9104
          resources:
            requests: { cpu: 100m, memory: 128Mi }
            limits: { cpu: 500m, memory: 256Mi }
---
apiVersion: v1
kind: Service
metadata:
  name: mysqld-exporter
  namespace: ruoyi-monitoring
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: mysqld-exporter
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
spec:
  ports:
    - name: metrics
      port: 9104
      targetPort: 9104
  selector:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: mysqld-exporter
---
apiVersion: monitoring.coreos.com/v1
kind: ServiceMonitor
metadata:
  name: mysqld-exporter
  namespace: ruoyi-monitoring
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
    release: kube-prometheus-stack
spec:
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app.kubernetes.io/name: mysqld-exporter
  endpoints:
    - port: metrics
      interval: 30s
```

> RDS 监控账号需具备 `PROCESS`、`REPLICATION CLIENT`、`SELECT` 权限：
> ```sql
> CREATE USER 'exporter'@'%' IDENTIFIED BY '<EXPORTER_PASSWORD>';
> GRANT PROCESS, REPLICATION CLIENT, SELECT ON *.* TO 'exporter'@'%';
> ```

**PodMonitor（无 Service 的场景，如 Filebeat/DaemonSet）**：

```yaml
apiVersion: monitoring.coreos.com/v1
kind: PodMonitor
metadata:
  name: ruoyi-filebeat
  namespace: ruoyi-monitoring
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
    release: kube-prometheus-stack
spec:
  namespaceSelector:
    matchNames: [ruoyi-logging]
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app.kubernetes.io/name: filebeat
  podMetricsEndpoints:
    - port: metrics
      path: /metrics
      interval: 60s
```

```bash
kubectl apply -f deploy/k8s/43-servicemonitors.yaml
kubectl -n ruoyi-monitoring get servicemonitor,podmonitor
# 预期: 列出全部 monitor
sleep 60
curl -s 'http://127.0.0.1:19090/api/v1/targets?state=active' \
  | jq -r '.data.activeTargets[]|select(.labels.job|test("ruoyi|elasticsearch|mysqld|nacos"))|"\(.labels.job)\t\(.health)\t\(.scrapeUrl)"'
# 预期: 全部 up
```

#### 6.3.3 Grafana 大盘导入编号清单

| # | 大盘名称 | Grafana.com ID | 用途 | 数据源 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | Kubernetes / Compute Resources / Cluster | 315 | 集群资源总览 | Prometheus |
| 2 | Kubernetes / Compute Resources / Namespace (Pods) | 6417 | 命名空间维度资源 | Prometheus |
| 3 | Kubernetes / Compute Resources / Node (Pods) | 6417（同盘切换） | 节点维度资源 | Prometheus |
| 4 | Kubernetes / Compute Resources / Pod | 6581 | 单 Pod 资源 | Prometheus |
| 5 | Kubernetes / Networking / Cluster | 6663 | 网络流量 | Prometheus |
| 6 | Kubernetes / Persistent Volumes | 13646 | PVC 容量与 IOPS | Prometheus |
| 7 | Node Exporter Full | 1860 | 节点系统指标 | Prometheus |
| 8 | JVM (Micrometer) | 4701 | JVM 堆/GC/线程 | Prometheus |
| 9 | Spring Boot 2.1 System Monitor | 11378 | Spring Boot 应用 | Prometheus |
| 10 | Nacos | 14498 | Nacos 注册配置中心 | Prometheus |
| 11 | Elasticsearch Exporter | 2322 | ES 集群/索引 | Prometheus |
| 12 | MySQL Overview | 7362 | MySQL 状态 | Prometheus |
| 13 | Redis Dashboard for Prometheus Redis Exporter | 763 | Redis 状态 | Prometheus |
| 14 | Nginx Ingress Controller | 9614 | Ingress 流量与延迟 | Prometheus |
| 15 | Alertmanager | 9578 | 告警概况 | Prometheus |
| 16 | Kubernetes Cluster Autoscaler | 3831 | 弹性伸缩 | Prometheus |
| 17 | SkyWalking OAP（自建） | — | OAP 自身指标（1234 端口） | Prometheus |

导入方式（CLI）：

```bash
GRAFANA=kube-prometheus-stack-grafana.ruoyi-monitoring.svc.cluster.local:80
USER=admin; PASS='<GRAFANA_ADMIN_PASSWORD>'

# 创建 Prometheus 数据源（若 helm 未自动创建）
curl -s -X POST "http://$GRAFANA/api/datasources" -u "$USER:$PASS" \
  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{
    "name":"Prometheus",
    "type":"prometheus",
    "url":"http://kube-prometheus-stack-prometheus.ruoyi-monitoring.svc.cluster.local:9090",
    "access":"proxy",
    "isDefault":true,
    "jsonData":{"timeInterval":"30s"}
  }'

# 批量导入大盘
for ID in 315 6417 6581 6663 13646 1860 4701 11378 14498 2322 7362 9614 9578; do
  curl -s -X POST "http://$GRAFANA/api/dashboards/import" -u "$USER:$PASS" \
    -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
    -d "{\"dashboard\":{\"id\":null,\"uid\":null},\"overwrite\":true,\"inputs\":[{\"name\":\"DS_PROMETHEUS\",\"type\":\"datasource\",\"pluginId\":\"prometheus\",\"value\":\"Prometheus\"}],\"folderId\":0,\"gnetId\":$ID}" \
    | jq -c "{id:$ID,status:.status,title:.title}"
  sleep 2
done
```

控制台导入路径：Grafana → Dashboards → **Import** → 输入 ID → Load → 选择 Prometheus 数据源 → Import。

验证：

```bash
curl -s "http://$GRAFANA/api/search?query=Kubernetes" -u "$USER:$PASS" | jq -r '.[].title'
# 预期: 列出已导入的 K8s 大盘
```

#### 6.3.4 告警规则 PromQL 示例

`deploy/k8s/44-prometheus-rules.yaml`：

```yaml
apiVersion: monitoring.coreos.com/v1
kind: PrometheusRule
metadata:
  name: ruoyi-custom-rules
  namespace: ruoyi-monitoring
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
    release: kube-prometheus-stack
spec:
  groups:
    # ==================== 应用层 ====================
    - name: ruoyi.application
      interval: 30s
      rules:
        # Pod 频繁重启
        - alert: RuoyiPodRestarting
          expr: |
            increase(kube_pod_container_status_restarts_total{namespace=~"ruoyi-.*"}[10m]) > 2
          for: 5m
          labels:
            severity: P2
            team: devops
          annotations:
            summary: "Pod {{ $labels.namespace }}/{{ $labels.pod }} 10 分钟内重启 {{ $value }} 次"
            description: "容器 {{ $labels.container }} 反复重启，请检查 OOM 或探针配置。"
            runbook: "https://wiki.example.com/runbook/pod-restart"

        # Pod 不可用（副本不足）
        - alert: RuoyiDeploymentReplicasMismatch
          expr: |
            kube_deployment_spec_replicas{namespace=~"ruoyi-.*"}
              != kube_deployment_status_replicas_available{namespace=~"ruoyi-.*"}
          for: 10m
          labels:
            severity: P1
          annotations:
            summary: "Deployment {{ $labels.namespace }}/{{ $labels.deployment }} 可用副本不足"
            description: "期望 {{ $value }}，实际可用少于期望，持续 10 分钟。"

        # 服务 5xx 比例过高
        - alert: RuoyiHighErrorRate
          expr: |
            sum by (service) (rate(http_server_requests_seconds_count{status=~"5..",namespace="ruoyi-prod"}[5m]))
              /
            sum by (service) (rate(http_server_requests_seconds_count{namespace="ruoyi-prod"}[5m]))
              > 0.01
          for: 5m
          labels:
            severity: P1
          annotations:
            summary: "服务 {{ $labels.service }} 5xx 比例 {{ $value | humanizePercentage }}"
            description: "5xx 比例超过 1% 持续 5 分钟，请检查下游依赖与日志。"

        # 接口 P99 延迟过高
        - alert: RuoyiHighP99Latency
          expr: |
            histogram_quantile(0.99,
              sum by (service, le) (rate(http_server_requests_seconds_bucket{namespace="ruoyi-prod"}[5m]))
            ) > 2
          for: 10m
          labels:
            severity: P2
          annotations:
            summary: "服务 {{ $labels.service }} P99 延迟 {{ $value }}s 超过 2s"
            description: "P99 持续 10 分钟超过阈值，请结合 SkyWalking 定位慢链路。"

        # JVM 堆使用率
        - alert: RuoyiJvmHeapHigh
          expr: |
            sum by (service, pod) (jvm_memory_used_bytes{area="heap",namespace="ruoyi-prod"})
              /
            sum by (service, pod) (jvm_memory_max_bytes{area="heap",namespace="ruoyi-prod"})
              > 0.85
          for: 10m
          labels:
            severity: P2
          annotations:
            summary: "{{ $labels.pod }} 堆内存使用率 {{ $value | humanizePercentage }}"
            description: "堆使用超过 85% 持续 10 分钟，存在 OOM 风险。"

        # JVM GC 停顿过长
        - alert: RuoyiJvmGcPauseHigh
          expr: |
            rate(jvm_gc_pause_seconds_sum{namespace="ruoyi-prod"}[5m])
              / rate(jvm_gc_pause_seconds_count{namespace="ruoyi-prod"}[5m]) > 0.5
          for: 10m
          labels:
            severity: P2
          annotations:
            summary: "{{ $labels.pod }} 平均 GC 停顿 {{ $value }}s"
            description: "GC 停顿过长，可能存在大对象或堆过小。"

        # 线程池/连接池接近耗尽（HikariCP）
        - alert: RuoyiDbPoolSaturated
          expr: |
            hikaricp_connections_active{namespace="ruoyi-prod"}
              / hikaricp_connections_max{namespace="ruoyi-prod"} > 0.9
          for: 5m
          labels:
            severity: P2
          annotations:
            summary: "{{ $labels.pod }} 数据库连接池使用率 {{ $value | humanizePercentage }}"
            description: "连接池接近耗尽，可能出现获取连接超时。"

        # Nacos 服务实例数异常
        - alert: RuoyiServiceInstanceDown
          expr: |
            count by (service) (up{job=~"ruoyi-.*",namespace="ruoyi-prod"}) < 1
          for: 3m
          labels:
            severity: P1
          annotations:
            summary: "服务 {{ $labels.service }} 无可抓取实例"
            description: "Prometheus 无法抓到该服务任何实例，可能全部宕机或 Service 异常。"

    # ==================== 资源层 ====================
    - name: ruoyi.resources
      interval: 30s
      rules:
        # CPU 限流（throttling）
        - alert: RuoyiCpuThrottling
          expr: |
            sum by (pod, namespace) (rate(container_cpu_cfs_throttled_periods_total{namespace=~"ruoyi-.*"}[5m]))
              /
            sum by (pod, namespace) (rate(container_cpu_cfs_periods_total{namespace=~"ruoyi-.*"}[5m]))
              > 0.25
          for: 10m
          labels:
            severity: P2
          annotations:
            summary: "{{ $labels.pod }} CPU 被限流 {{ $value | humanizePercentage }}"
            description: "CPU 限流比例超过 25%，需上调 limits 或扩容副本。"

        # 容器内存接近 limit
        - alert: RuoyiContainerMemoryHigh
          expr: |
            sum by (pod, namespace) (container_memory_working_set_bytes{namespace=~"ruoyi-.*",container!="",container!="POD"})
              /
            sum by (pod, namespace) (kube_pod_container_resource_limits{namespace=~"ruoyi-.*",resource="memory"})
              > 0.9
          for: 10m
          labels:
            severity: P2
          annotations:
            summary: "{{ $labels.pod }} 内存使用接近 limit（{{ $value | humanizePercentage }}）"
            description: "接近 limit 会被 OOMKilled，请调整 JVM_XMX 或 limits。"

        # 节点 CPU 高
        - alert: RuoyiNodeCpuHigh
          expr: |
            100 - (avg by (instance) (rate(node_cpu_seconds_total{mode="idle"}[5m])) * 100) > 85
          for: 15m
          labels:
            severity: P2
          annotations:
            summary: "节点 {{ $labels.instance }} CPU 使用率 {{ $value }}%"
            description: "节点 CPU 持续高负载，考虑扩容节点池。"

        # 节点内存高
        - alert: RuoyiNodeMemoryHigh
          expr: |
            (1 - node_memory_MemAvailable_bytes / node_memory_MemTotal_bytes) > 0.9
          for: 15m
          labels:
            severity: P1
          annotations:
            summary: "节点 {{ $labels.instance }} 内存使用率 {{ $value | humanizePercentage }}"
            description: "节点内存不足，可能触发 OOM 驱逐。"

        # 节点磁盘空间不足
        - alert: RuoyiNodeDiskSpaceLow
          expr: |
            (1 - node_filesystem_avail_bytes{fstype!~"tmpfs|overlay"} / node_filesystem_size_bytes{fstype!~"tmpfs|overlay"}) > 0.85
          for: 15m
          labels:
            severity: P1
          annotations:
            summary: "节点 {{ $labels.instance }} 磁盘 {{ $labels.mountpoint }} 使用率 {{ $value | humanizePercentage }}"
            description: "磁盘空间不足，可能导致 Pod 驱逐或 ES 索引只读。"

        # PVC 使用率
        - alert: RuoyiPvcUsageHigh
          expr: |
            kubelet_volume_stats_used_bytes{namespace=~"ruoyi-.*"}
              / kubelet_volume_stats_capacity_bytes{namespace=~"ruoyi-.*"} > 0.85
          for: 15m
          labels:
            severity: P2
          annotations:
            summary: "PVC {{ $labels.persistentvolumeclaim }} 使用率 {{ $value | humanizePercentage }}"
            description: "存储接近满，需扩容 PVC 或清理数据。"

    # ==================== 存储与中间件 ====================
    - name: ruoyi.middleware
      interval: 30s
      rules:
        # ES 集群状态非 green
        - alert: RuoyiEsClusterNotGreen
          expr: |
            elasticsearch_cluster_health_status{color="green"} == 0
          for: 5m
          labels:
            severity: P1
          annotations:
            summary: "Elasticsearch 集群状态非 green"
            description: "当前状态 {{ $labels.color }}，请检查分片分配与节点健康。"

        # ES 未分配分片
        - alert: RuoyiEsUnassignedShards
          expr: |
            elasticsearch_cluster_health_unassigned_shards > 0
          for: 10m
          labels:
            severity: P2
          annotations:
            summary: "Elasticsearch 有 {{ $value }} 个未分配分片"
            description: "未分配分片会降低可用性与查询性能。"

        # ES JVM 堆压力
        - alert: RuoyiEsJvmHeapHigh
          expr: |
            elasticsearch_jvm_memory_used_bytes{area="heap"} / elasticsearch_jvm_memory_max_bytes{area="heap"} > 0.85
          for: 10m
          labels:
            severity: P2
          annotations:
            summary: "ES 节点 {{ $labels.node }} 堆使用率 {{ $value | humanizePercentage }}"
            description: "ES 堆压力大，可能触发长时间 GC 甚至拒绝请求。"

        # Nacos 节点不可用
        - alert: RuoyiNacosNodeDown
          expr: |
            up{job="ruoyi-nacos"} == 0
          for: 3m
          labels:
            severity: P1
          annotations:
            summary: "Nacos 节点 {{ $labels.instance }} 不可用"
            description: "Nacos 集群节点异常，低于多数派将影响服务发现。"

        # MySQL 连接数接近上限
        - alert: RuoyiMysqlConnectionHigh
          expr: |
            mysql_global_status_threads_connected / mysql_global_variables_max_connections > 0.8
          for: 10m
          labels:
            severity: P2
          annotations:
            summary: "MySQL 连接数使用率 {{ $value | humanizePercentage }}"
            description: "连接数接近上限，需检查连接池配置或上调 max_connections。"

        # MySQL 慢查询增多
        - alert: RuoyiMysqlSlowQueries
          expr: |
            rate(mysql_global_status_slow_queries[5m]) > 1
          for: 10m
          labels:
            severity: P3
          annotations:
            summary: "MySQL 慢查询速率 {{ $value }}/s"
            description: "慢查询增多，需结合慢日志优化索引。"

    # ==================== 网关与可用性 ====================
    - name: ruoyi.availability
      interval: 30s
      rules:
        # 网关 PDB 被阻塞（节点驱逐受阻）
        - alert: RuoyiPdbBlocked
          expr: |
            kube_poddisruptionbudget_status_pod_disruptions_allowed < 1
          for: 30m
          labels:
            severity: P3
          annotations:
            summary: "PDB {{ $labels.poddisruptionbudget }} 阻止了 Pod 驱逐"
            description: "长时间无可用驱逐配额，会影响节点维护与升级。"

        # HPA 达到最大副本（容量瓶颈）
        - alert: RuoyiHpaAtMax
          expr: |
            kube_horizontalpodautoscaler_status_current_replicas
              == kube_horizontalpodautoscaler_spec_max_replicas
          for: 20m
          labels:
            severity: P2
          annotations:
            summary: "HPA {{ $labels.horizontalpodautoscaler }} 已达最大副本"
            description: "服务已扩到上限，可能成为容量瓶颈，需上调 maxReplicas。"

        # 证书 30 天内过期
        - alert: RuoyiCertExpiringSoon
          expr: |
            (probe_ssl_earliest_cert_expiry - time()) / 86400 < 30
          for: 1h
          labels:
            severity: P2
          annotations:
            summary: "证书 {{ $labels.instance }} 将在 {{ $value }} 天后过期"
            description: "请检查 cert-manager 续期状态或手工更新证书。"
```

```bash
kubectl apply -f deploy/k8s/44-prometheus-rules.yaml
sleep 60
curl -s 'http://127.0.0.1:19090/api/v1/rules?type=alert' \
  | jq -r '.data.groups[]|select(.name|test("ruoyi"))|.rules[]|"\(.name)\t\(.state)\t\(.health)"'
# 预期: 列出全部自定义规则，health=ok
```

**在 Grafana 中查看告警**：Alerting → Alert rules → 应能看到自定义规则；Alerting → Contact points 配置通知渠道（见 6.5）。

**PromQL 速查（排查时常用）**：

| 目的 | PromQL |
| --- | --- |
| 某服务 QPS | `sum by (service) (rate(http_server_requests_seconds_count{namespace="ruoyi-prod"}[5m]))` |
| 某服务 P99 | `histogram_quantile(0.99, sum by (le,service) (rate(http_server_requests_seconds_bucket{namespace="ruoyi-prod"}[5m])))` |
| 某服务 5xx 速率 | `sum by (service) (rate(http_server_requests_seconds_count{status=~"5..",namespace="ruoyi-prod"}[5m]))` |
| Pod CPU 使用（核） | `sum by (pod) (rate(container_cpu_usage_seconds_total{namespace="ruoyi-prod",container!=""}[5m]))` |
| Pod 内存使用（MiB） | `sum by (pod) (container_memory_working_set_bytes{namespace="ruoyi-prod",container!=""}) / 1024 / 1024` |
| JVM 堆使用率 | `sum by (pod) (jvm_memory_used_bytes{area="heap"}) / sum by (pod) (jvm_memory_max_bytes{area="heap"})` |
| GC 次数/秒 | `sum by (pod) (rate(jvm_gc_pause_seconds_count[5m]))` |
| 活跃线程数 | `jvm_threads_live_threads{namespace="ruoyi-prod"}` |
| Hikari 连接池 | `hikaricp_connections_active{namespace="ruoyi-prod"}` |
| 节点可用内存 | `node_memory_MemAvailable_bytes / 1024/1024/1024` |
| 容器重启次数（近 1h） | `increase(kube_pod_container_status_restarts_total{namespace="ruoyi-prod"}[1h])` |

### 6.4 日志 ELK

#### 6.4.1 Elasticsearch 3 节点集群部署

**方案 A：云 ES（推荐）**

```text
控制台：Elasticsearch 控制台 → 创建实例
  地域：华东1（杭州）；版本：7.17.21（与 Filebeat/Kibana 严格一致）
  规格：8C32G × 3 节点（生产建议）；存储：ESSD PL2 500GiB/节点
  专有网络：vpc-ruoyi-prod；交换机：vsw-ruoyi-h/i/j
  账号：elastic / <ES_PASSWORD>
  白名单：172.16.1.0/24、172.16.2.0/24、172.16.3.0/24
  插件：开启 Kibana（版本需一致）
```

**方案 B：K8s 自建 ES 3 节点（StatefulSet）**

```yaml
apiVersion: v1
kind: Service
metadata:
  name: ruoyi-elasticsearch
  namespace: ruoyi-logging
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-elasticsearch
    app.kubernetes.io/instance: ruoyi-elasticsearch
    app.kubernetes.io/version: "7.17.21"
    app.kubernetes.io/component: log-storage
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
    app.kubernetes.io/managed-by: Helm
spec:
  clusterIP: None
  publishNotReadyAddresses: true
  ports:
    - name: http
      port: 9200
      targetPort: 9200
    - name: transport
      port: 9300
      targetPort: 9300
    - name: metrics
      port: 9114
      targetPort: 9114
  selector:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-elasticsearch
    app.kubernetes.io/instance: ruoyi-elasticsearch
---
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: StatefulSet
metadata:
  name: ruoyi-elasticsearch
  namespace: ruoyi-logging
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-elasticsearch
    app.kubernetes.io/instance: ruoyi-elasticsearch
    app.kubernetes.io/version: "7.17.21"
    app.kubernetes.io/component: log-storage
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
    app.kubernetes.io/managed-by: Helm
spec:
  serviceName: ruoyi-elasticsearch
  replicas: 3
  podManagementPolicy: Parallel
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-elasticsearch
      app.kubernetes.io/instance: ruoyi-elasticsearch
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-elasticsearch
        app.kubernetes.io/instance: ruoyi-elasticsearch
        app.kubernetes.io/version: "7.17.21"
        app.kubernetes.io/component: log-storage
        app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
        app.kubernetes.io/managed-by: Helm
    spec:
      serviceAccountName: default
      imagePullSecrets:
        - name: ruoyi-acr-secret
      terminationGracePeriodSeconds: 120
      securityContext:
        runAsNonRoot: true
        runAsUser: 1000
        fsGroup: 1000
      # ---------- ES 必须关闭 THP、提升 mmap ----------
      initContainers:
        - name: sysctl
          image: <YOUR_ACR_REGISTRY>/ruoyi-prod/rocky-tools:9.3
          command: ["sh", "-c", "sysctl -w vm.max_map_count=262144"]
          securityContext:
            privileged: true
            runAsUser: 0
      topologySpreadConstraints:
        - maxSkew: 1
          topologyKey: topology.kubernetes.io/zone
          whenUnsatisfiable: DoNotSchedule
          labelSelector:
            matchLabels:
              app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-elasticsearch
      # ES 对磁盘延迟极敏感，强制一节点一 Pod
      affinity:
        podAntiAffinity:
          requiredDuringSchedulingIgnoredDuringExecution:
            - topologyKey: kubernetes.io/hostname
              labelSelector:
                matchLabels:
                  app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-elasticsearch
      nodeSelector:
        node-pool: logging
      tolerations:
        - key: dedicated
          operator: Equal
          value: logging
          effect: NoSchedule
      containers:
        - name: elasticsearch
          image: <YOUR_ACR_REGISTRY>/ruoyi-prod/elasticsearch:7.17.21
          imagePullPolicy: IfNotPresent
          ports:
            - name: http
              containerPort: 9200
            - name: transport
              containerPort: 9300
          env:
            - name: cluster.name
              value: ruoyi-es-prod
            - name: node.name
              valueFrom:
                fieldRef: { fieldPath: metadata.name }
            - name: discovery.seed_hosts
              value: "ruoyi-elasticsearch-0.ruoyi-elasticsearch.ruoyi-logging.svc.cluster.local,ruoyi-elasticsearch-1.ruoyi-elasticsearch.ruoyi-logging.svc.cluster.local,ruoyi-elasticsearch-2.ruoyi-elasticsearch.ruoyi-logging.svc.cluster.local"
            - name: cluster.initial_master_nodes
              value: "ruoyi-elasticsearch-0,ruoyi-elasticsearch-1,ruoyi-elasticsearch-2"
            # 三节点全角色（生产小集群常见做法；大集群应拆分 master/data）
            - name: node.roles
              value: "master,data,ingest,ml,remote_cluster_client,transform"
            - name: bootstrap.memory_lock
              value: "true"
            - name: ES_JAVA_OPTS
              value: "-Xms16g -Xmx16g -XX:+UseG1GC -XX:MaxGCPauseMillis=200 -XX:+HeapDumpOnOutOfMemoryError -XX:HeapDumpPath=/usr/share/elasticsearch/data"
            - name: xpack.security.enabled
              value: "false"     # 内网 + NetworkPolicy；云 ES 方案下必须为 true
            - name: xpack.ml.enabled
              value: "false"
            - name: indices.query.bool.max_clause_count
              value: "4096"
            - name: TZ
              value: Asia/Shanghai
          resources:
            requests:
              cpu: 4000m
              memory: 16Gi
            limits:
              cpu: "8"
              memory: 32Gi
          securityContext:
            capabilities:
              add: ["IPC_LOCK"]
          readinessProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /_cluster/health?local=true
              port: 9200
            initialDelaySeconds: 30
            periodSeconds: 10
            timeoutSeconds: 5
            failureThreshold: 10
          livenessProbe:
            tcpSocket: { port: 9200 }
            initialDelaySeconds: 60
            periodSeconds: 20
            failureThreshold: 5
          volumeMounts:
            - name: es-data
              mountPath: /usr/share/elasticsearch/data
            - name: es-config
              mountPath: /usr/share/elasticsearch/config/elasticsearch.yml
              subPath: elasticsearch.yml
              readOnly: true
      volumes:
        - name: es-config
          configMap:
            name: ruoyi-es-config
  volumeClaimTemplates:
    - metadata:
        name: es-data
        labels:
          app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-elasticsearch
          app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
      spec:
        accessModes: ["ReadWriteOnce"]
        storageClassName: alicloud-disk-essd-pl2
        resources:
          requests:
            storage: 500Gi
---
apiVersion: v1
kind: ConfigMap
metadata:
  name: ruoyi-es-config
  namespace: ruoyi-logging
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-elasticsearch
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
data:
  elasticsearch.yml: |
    cluster.name: ruoyi-es-prod
    network.host: 0.0.0.0
    http.port: 9200
    transport.port: 9300
    # 生产必须显式声明单节点角色与发现配置
    discovery.seed_hosts: ["ruoyi-elasticsearch-0.ruoyi-elasticsearch.ruoyi-logging.svc.cluster.local","ruoyi-elasticsearch-1.ruoyi-elasticsearch.ruoyi-logging.svc.cluster.local","ruoyi-elasticsearch-2.ruoyi-elasticsearch.ruoyi-logging.svc.cluster.local"]
    cluster.initial_master_nodes: ["ruoyi-elasticsearch-0","ruoyi-elasticsearch-1","ruoyi-elasticsearch-2"]
    # 防止脑裂：最小主节点数 = (master 数 / 2) + 1
    discovery.zen.minimum_master_nodes: 2
    # 分片恢复限速，避免大量恢复打爆磁盘
    cluster.routing.allocation.node_concurrent_recoveries: 2
    indices.recovery.max_bytes_per_sec: 100mb
    # 写入压力控制
    thread_pool.write.queue_size: 2000
    # 磁盘水位（85% 后不再分配新分片）
    cluster.routing.allocation.disk.threshold_enabled: true
    cluster.routing.allocation.disk.watermark.low: 85%
    cluster.routing.allocation.disk.watermark.high: 90%
    cluster.routing.allocation.disk.watermark.flood_stage: 95%
    # 仅允许内网访问
    http.cors.enabled: false
    xpack.security.enabled: false
    xpack.ml.enabled: false
    action.destructive_requires_name: true
    reindex.remote.whitelist: []
```

```bash
kubectl apply -f deploy/k8s/45-elasticsearch.yaml
kubectl -n ruoyi-logging get pods -l app.kubernetes.io/name=ruoyi-elasticsearch -w
# 预期: 3 个 Pod Running（首次启动 1-3 分钟）
kubectl -n ruoyi-logging exec ruoyi-elasticsearch-0 -- \
  curl -s "http://127.0.0.1:9200/_cluster/health?pretty"
# 预期: status=green, number_of_nodes=3, unassigned_shards=0
kubectl -n ruoyi-logging exec ruoyi-elasticsearch-0 -- \
  curl -s "http://127.0.0.1:9200/_cat/nodes?v&h=name,ip,node.role,master,heap.percent,disk.used_percent"
```

失败判据：

| 现象 | 原因 | 处理 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `max virtual memory areas vm.max_map_count [65530] is too low` | sysctl 未生效 | 确认 initContainer 已执行；节点基线 2.3.2 |
| `failed to obtain node locks` | 数据目录被占用或权限错 | 检查 PVC 与 fsGroup |
| Pod 反复重启，`circuit_breaking_exception` | 堆太小 | 提升 `ES_JAVA_OPTS` 与 limits（堆 ≤ 物理内存 50%） |
| `status=yellow` 且 unassigned=3 | 副本无法分配（单节点场景） | 3 节点场景不应出现；检查分片数与节点数 |
| `index read-only` | 磁盘超 flood_stage | 临时解除：`PUT _all/_settings {"index.blocks.read_only_allow_delete": null}` 后清理数据 |

#### 6.4.2 索引模板与 ILM 策略

**索引模板**（`deploy/elk/index-template-ruoyi.json`）：

```json
{
  "index_patterns": ["ruoyi-logs-*"],
  "order": 100,
  "version": 1,
  "settings": {
    "index": {
      "number_of_shards": "3",
      "number_of_replicas": "1",
      "refresh_interval": "10s",
      "translog.durability": "async",
      "translog.sync_interval": "10s",
      "codec": "best_compression",
      "mapping.total_fields.limit": "2000",
      "max_result_window": "50000"
    }
  },
  "mappings": {
    "dynamic_templates": [
      {
        "strings_as_keyword": {
          "match_mapping_type": "string",
          "mapping": { "type": "keyword", "ignore_above": 1024 }
        }
      }
    ],
    "properties": {
      "@timestamp":       { "type": "date" },
      "time":             { "type": "date" },
      "level":            { "type": "keyword" },
      "logger_name":      { "type": "keyword", "ignore_above": 512 },
      "message":          { "type": "text", "analyzer": "standard" },
      "stack_trace":      { "type": "text" },
      "thread_name":      { "type": "keyword" },
      "trace_id":         { "type": "keyword" },
      "tid":              { "type": "keyword" },
      "span_id":          { "type": "keyword" },
      "app":              { "type": "keyword" },
      "service":          { "type": "keyword" },
      "kubernetes": {
        "properties": {
          "namespace":  { "type": "keyword" },
          "pod":        { "type": "keyword" },
          "node":       { "type": "keyword" },
          "container":  { "type": "keyword" },
          "labels": {
            "properties": {
              "app_kubernetes_io_name":      { "type": "keyword" },
              "app_kubernetes_io_version":   { "type": "keyword" },
              "app_kubernetes_io_component": { "type": "keyword" },
              "node_pool":                   { "type": "keyword" },
              "topology_kubernetes_io_zone": { "type": "keyword" }
            }
          }
        }
      },
      "host": { "properties": { "name": { "type": "keyword" } } },
      "log":  { "properties": { "file": { "properties": { "path": { "type": "keyword" } } } } },
      "stream": { "type": "keyword" }
    }
  }
}
```

**ILM 策略**（`deploy/elk/ilm-ruoyi-logs.json`）：

```json
{
  "policy": {
    "phases": {
      "hot": {
        "min_age": "0ms",
        "actions": {
          "rollover": {
            "max_primary_shard_size": "30gb",
            "max_age": "1d",
            "max_docs": 100000000
          },
          "set_priority": { "priority": 100 }
        }
      },
      "warm": {
        "min_age": "3d",
        "actions": {
          "set_priority": { "priority": 50 },
          "forcemerge": { "max_num_segments": 1 },
          "shrink": { "number_of_shards": 1 },
          "allocate": { "number_of_replicas": 1 }
        }
      },
      "cold": {
        "min_age": "15d",
        "actions": {
          "set_priority": { "priority": 0 },
          "allocate": { "number_of_replicas": 1 }
        }
      },
      "delete": {
        "min_age": "30d",
        "actions": { "delete": {} }
      }
    }
  }
}
```

| 阶段 | 天数 | 分片数 | 副本 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| hot | 0–3 天 | 3 | 1 | 写入与近期查询 |
| warm | 3–15 天 | 1（shrink） | 1 | 合并段，减少资源 |
| cold | 15–30 天 | 1 | 1 | 低频查询 |
| delete | 30 天 | — | — | 自动删除 |

> **合规提示**：若审计要求日志保留 ≥180 天，把 delete 改为 `min_age: 180d`，或 cold 阶段后用 Snapshot 存 OSS（7.2.3）再删除。**变更该值需在变更单中说明**。

应用模板与策略：

```bash
ES=ruoyi-elasticsearch.ruoyi-logging.svc.cluster.local:9200

# 1) 上传 ILM 策略
curl -s -X PUT "http://$ES/_ilm/policy/ruoyi-logs-policy" \
  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d @deploy/elk/ilm-ruoyi-logs.json | jq

# 2) 上传索引模板（含 ILM 关联与 rollover alias）
jq '.settings.index.lifecycle = {"name":"ruoyi-logs-policy","rollover_alias":"ruoyi-logs"}' \
  deploy/elk/index-template-ruoyi.json > /tmp/tpl.json
curl -s -X PUT "http://$ES/_index_template/ruoyi-logs-template" \
  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d @/tmp/tpl.json | jq

# 3) 创建引导索引（rollover 需要）
curl -s -X PUT "http://$ES/ruoyi-logs-000001" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{
  "aliases": { "ruoyi-logs": { "is_write_index": true } }
}' | jq

# 4) 验证
curl -s "http://$ES/_ilm/policy/ruoyi-logs-policy" | jq '.ruoyi-logs-policy.policy.phases|keys'
curl -s "http://$ES/_index_template/ruoyi-logs-template" | jq '.index_templates[0].name'
curl -s "http://$ES/_cat/aliases/ruoyi-logs?v"
# 预期: 策略含 hot/warm/cold/delete；模板名正确；alias 指向 000001 且 is_write_index=true
```

#### 6.4.3 Kibana 部署与访问

```yaml
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: ruoyi-kibana
  namespace: ruoyi-logging
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-kibana
    app.kubernetes.io/instance: ruoyi-kibana
    app.kubernetes.io/version: "7.17.21"
    app.kubernetes.io/component: log-ui
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
    app.kubernetes.io/managed-by: Helm
spec:
  replicas: 2
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-kibana
      app.kubernetes.io/instance: ruoyi-kibana
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-kibana
        app.kubernetes.io/instance: ruoyi-kibana
        app.kubernetes.io/version: "7.17.21"
        app.kubernetes.io/component: log-ui
        app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
        app.kubernetes.io/managed-by: Helm
    spec:
      imagePullSecrets:
        - name: ruoyi-acr-secret
      securityContext:
        runAsNonRoot: true
        runAsUser: 1000
        fsGroup: 1000
      nodeSelector:
        node-pool: logging
      tolerations:
        - key: dedicated
          operator: Equal
          value: logging
          effect: NoSchedule
      containers:
        - name: kibana
          image: <YOUR_ACR_REGISTRY>/ruoyi-prod/kibana:7.17.21
          imagePullPolicy: IfNotPresent
          ports:
            - name: http
              containerPort: 5601
          env:
            - name: ELASTICSEARCH_HOSTS
              value: "http://ruoyi-elasticsearch.ruoyi-logging.svc.cluster.local:9200"
            - name: SERVER_NAME
              value: "kibana.example.com"
            - name: SERVER_HOST
              value: "0.0.0.0"
            - name: SERVER_PUBLICBASEURL
              value: "https://kibana.example.com"
            - name: SERVER_BASEPATH
              value: ""
            - name: TELEMETRY_ENABLED
              value: "false"
            - name: MONITORING_ENABLED
              value: "false"
            - name: I18N_LOCALE
              value: "zh-CN"
            - name: NODE_OPTIONS
              value: "--max-old-space-size=2048"
            - name: TZ
              value: Asia/Shanghai
          resources:
            requests: { cpu: 500m, memory: 1Gi }
            limits:   { cpu: "1",  memory: 2Gi }
          startupProbe:
            httpGet: { path: /api/status, port: 5601 }
            initialDelaySeconds: 30
            periodSeconds: 10
            failureThreshold: 30
          readinessProbe:
            httpGet: { path: /api/status, port: 5601 }
            initialDelaySeconds: 20
            periodSeconds: 10
            failureThreshold: 3
          livenessProbe:
            httpGet: { path: /api/status, port: 5601 }
            initialDelaySeconds: 60
            periodSeconds: 20
            failureThreshold: 4
---
apiVersion: v1
kind: Service
metadata:
  name: ruoyi-kibana
  namespace: ruoyi-logging
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-kibana
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
spec:
  type: ClusterIP
  ports:
    - name: http
      port: 5601
      targetPort: 5601
  selector:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-kibana
    app.kubernetes.io/instance: ruoyi-kibana
```

```bash
kubectl apply -f deploy/k8s/46-kibana.yaml
kubectl -n ruoyi-logging rollout status deploy/ruoyi-kibana --timeout=600s
kubectl -n ruoyi-logging port-forward svc/ruoyi-kibana 15601:5601 &
curl -s http://127.0.0.1:15601/api/status | jq '.status.overall.level'
# 预期: "available"
kill %1
```

访问方式：`https://kibana.example.com`（经 5.3.1 的 `ruoyi-console` Ingress，含 Basic Auth + IP 白名单）。
Kibana 首次使用需创建 Index Pattern：

```bash
# 通过 API 创建 Index Pattern（Kibana 7.17）
KIBANA=ruoyi-kibana.ruoyi-logging.svc.cluster.local:5601
curl -s -X POST "http://$KIBANA/api/saved_objects/index-pattern/ruoyi-logs-*" \
  -H 'kbn-xsrf: true' -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -d '{"attributes":{"title":"ruoyi-logs-*","timeFieldName":"@timestamp"}}' | jq '.id'
# 预期: "ruoyi-logs-*"
```

#### 6.4.4 Filebeat DaemonSet 完整 YAML

`deploy/k8s/47-filebeat.yaml`：

```yaml
# ==================== ServiceAccount + RBAC ====================
apiVersion: v1
kind: ServiceAccount
metadata:
  name: filebeat
  namespace: ruoyi-logging
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: filebeat
    app.kubernetes.io/version: "7.17.21"
    app.kubernetes.io/component: log-collector
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
    app.kubernetes.io/managed-by: Helm
---
apiVersion: rbac.authorization.k8s.io/v1
kind: ClusterRole
metadata:
  name: ruoyi-filebeat
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: filebeat
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
rules:
  - apiGroups: [""]
    resources: ["namespaces", "pods", "nodes"]
    verbs: ["get", "list", "watch"]
  - apiGroups: ["apps"]
    resources: ["replicasets", "deployments", "statefulsets", "daemonsets"]
    verbs: ["get", "list", "watch"]
  - apiGroups: [""]
    resources: ["events"]
    verbs: ["get", "list", "watch"]
---
apiVersion: rbac.authorization.k8s.io/v1
kind: ClusterRoleBinding
metadata:
  name: ruoyi-filebeat
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: filebeat
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
roleRef:
  apiGroup: rbac.authorization.k8s.io
  kind: ClusterRole
  name: ruoyi-filebeat
subjects:
  - kind: ServiceAccount
    name: filebeat
    namespace: ruoyi-logging
---
# ==================== ConfigMap（filebeat.yml）====================
apiVersion: v1
kind: ConfigMap
metadata:
  name: filebeat-config
  namespace: ruoyi-logging
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: filebeat
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
data:
  filebeat.yml: |
    # ==================== 全局 ====================
    name: ${NODE_NAME}
    tags: ["ruoyi", "kubernetes"]
    # 采集器自身限制，避免抢占业务资源
    max_procs: 2
    queue.mem:
      events: 8192
      flush.min_events: 1024
      flush.timeout: 2s
    filebeat.shutdown_timeout: 30s

    # ==================== 采集：容器 stdout 日志 ====================
    filebeat.inputs:
      - type: container
        enabled: true
        # 只采集 ruoyi-* 命名空间
        paths:
          - /var/log/pods/ruoyi-prod_*/*/*.log
          - /var/log/pods/ruoyi-middleware_*/*/*.log
          - /var/log/pods/ruoyi-monitoring_*/*/*.log
          - /var/log/pods/ruoyi-logging_*/*/*.log
          - /var/log/pods/ruoyi-ingress_*/*/*.log
        # 排除 Filebeat 自身，避免自噬
        exclude_files: ['filebeat']
        # ------------- 多行合并（Java 堆栈） -------------
        multiline:
          type: pattern
          pattern: '^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}[ T][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}'
          negate: true
          match: after
          max_lines: 500
          timeout: 5s
        # ------------- 容器日志为 CRI 格式，需解析 -------------
        processors:
          - add_kubernetes_metadata:
              host: ${NODE_NAME}
              in_cluster: true
              default_indexers.enabled: false
              default_matchers.enabled: false
              indexers:
                - pod_name
              matchers:
                - logs_path:
                    logs_path: "/var/log/pods/"
          - decode_json_fields:
              fields: ["message"]
              target: ""
              overwrite_keys: true
              add_error_key: true
              process_array: false
              max_depth: 3
          - add_fields:
              target: ""
              fields:
                cluster: ack-ruoyi-prod
                region: cn-hangzhou

    # ==================== 自动发现（补充 Kubernetes 元数据 + 按注解采集）====================
    filebeat.autodiscover:
      providers:
        - type: kubernetes
          host: ${NODE_NAME}
          in_cluster: true
          node: ${NODE_NAME}
          hints.enabled: true
          hints.default_config:
            type: container
            paths:
              - /var/log/containers/*${data.kubernetes.container.id}.log
            multiline:
              type: pattern
              pattern: '^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}'
              negate: true
              match: after
          # 仅处理 ruoyi 命名空间
          templates:
            - condition:
                or:
                  - equals:
                      kubernetes.namespace: ruoyi-prod
                  - equals:
                      kubernetes.namespace: ruoyi-middleware
                  - equals:
                      kubernetes.namespace: ruoyi-monitoring
                  - equals:
                      kubernetes.namespace: ruoyi-logging
              config:
                - type: container
                  paths:
                    - /var/log/containers/*${data.kubernetes.container.id}.log
                  multiline:
                    type: pattern
                    pattern: '^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}'
                    negate: true
                    match: after
                    max_lines: 500

    # ==================== 字段处理 ====================
    processors:
      - add_host_metadata:
          when.not.contains.tags: forwarded
      - add_cloud_metadata: ~
      - add_kubernetes_metadata:
          host: ${NODE_NAME}
          in_cluster: true
      # 从 JSON 日志中提取 traceId 到顶层，便于检索与关联
      - rename:
          fields:
            - from: "tid"
              to: "trace_id"
          ignore_missing: true
          fail_on_error: false
      - rename:
          fields:
            - from: "level"
              to: "log_level"
          ignore_missing: true
          fail_on_error: false
      - copy:
          fields:
            - from: "log_level"
              to: "level"
          ignore_missing: true
          fail_on_error: false
      # 归一化日志级别（避免小写/大写不一致）
      - uppercase:
          field: "level"
          ignore_missing: true
      - drop_fields:
          fields: ["agent.ephemeral_id", "ecs.version", "input.type", "container.image.name"]
          ignore_missing: true
      # 丢弃健康检查/探针噪声，降低 ES 压力
      - drop_event:
          when:
            or:
              - regexp:
                  message: "actuator/(health|liveness|readiness|prometheus)"
              - regexp:
                  request_uri: "^/(healthz|favicon.ico)"
      # 慢查询/异常日志打标签，便于 Kibana 快速筛选
      - add_tags:
          tags: ["has_stacktrace"]
          when:
            regexp:
              message: "(Exception|Caused by:|\\bat com\\.)"
      - add_tags:
          tags: ["error"]
          when:
            or:
              - equals: { level: "ERROR" }
              - equals: { level: "FATAL" }

    # ==================== 输出到 ES ====================
    output.elasticsearch:
      hosts: ["http://ruoyi-elasticsearch.ruoyi-logging.svc.cluster.local:9200"]
      # 直接写入 rollover alias，由 ILM 管理索引滚动
      index: "ruoyi-logs"
      # 每日索引 + ILM
      ilm.enabled: true
      ilm.rollover_alias: "ruoyi-logs"
      ilm.pattern: "{now/d}-000001"
      # 写入性能
      bulk_max_size: 2048
      worker: 2
      compression_level: 3
      backoff.init: 1s
      backoff.max: 60s
      # 只保留必要字段，控制索引体积
      pipeline: "ruoyi-log-pipeline"

    # ==================== 监控 ====================
    http.enabled: true
    http.host: 0.0.0.0
    http.port: 5066
    logging.level: info
    logging.to_files: false

    # ==================== 其它 ====================
    setup.template.enabled: false     # 模板由 6.4.2 手工管理，避免被覆盖
    setup.ilm.enabled: true
    setup.ilm.overwrite: false
---
# ==================== DaemonSet ====================
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: DaemonSet
metadata:
  name: filebeat
  namespace: ruoyi-logging
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: filebeat
    app.kubernetes.io/instance: filebeat
    app.kubernetes.io/version: "7.17.21"
    app.kubernetes.io/component: log-collector
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
    app.kubernetes.io/managed-by: Helm
spec:
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app.kubernetes.io/name: filebeat
  updateStrategy:
    type: RollingUpdate
    rollingUpdate:
      maxUnavailable: 1
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app.kubernetes.io/name: filebeat
        app.kubernetes.io/instance: filebeat
        app.kubernetes.io/version: "7.17.21"
        app.kubernetes.io/component: log-collector
        app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
        app.kubernetes.io/managed-by: Helm
      annotations:
        # 让 Prometheus 抓取自监控指标
        prometheus.io/scrape: "true"
        prometheus.io/port: "5066"
        prometheus.io/path: "/metrics"
    spec:
      serviceAccountName: filebeat
      imagePullSecrets:
        - name: ruoyi-acr-secret
      terminationGracePeriodSeconds: 60
      # 采集节点全部日志，必须容忍所有污点
      tolerations:
        - operator: Exists
          effect: NoSchedule
        - operator: Exists
          effect: NoExecute
      hostNetwork: false
      dnsPolicy: ClusterFirstWithHostNet
      securityContext:
        runAsUser: 0        # 读取 /var/log/pods 需要 root（或配置正确的 fsGroup）
      containers:
        - name: filebeat
          image: <YOUR_ACR_REGISTRY>/ruoyi-prod/filebeat:7.17.21
          imagePullPolicy: IfNotPresent
          args:
            - "-c"
            - "/etc/filebeat.yml"
            - "-e"
            - "-strict.perms=false"
          env:
            - name: NODE_NAME
              valueFrom:
                fieldRef:
                  fieldPath: spec.nodeName
            - name: POD_NAME
              valueFrom:
                fieldRef:
                  fieldPath: metadata.name
            - name: TZ
              value: Asia/Shanghai
          resources:
            requests:
              cpu: 100m
              memory: 200Mi
            limits:
              cpu: 500m
              memory: 500Mi
          securityContext:
            runAsUser: 0
            allowPrivilegeEscalation: false
            capabilities:
              drop: ["ALL"]
          volumeMounts:
            - name: config
              mountPath: /etc/filebeat.yml
              subPath: filebeat.yml
              readOnly: true
            - name: data
              mountPath: /usr/share/filebeat/data
            - name: varlogpods
              mountPath: /var/log/pods
              readOnly: true
            - name: varlogcontainers
              mountPath: /var/log/containers
              readOnly: true
            - name: varlibdockercontainers
              mountPath: /var/lib/docker/containers
              readOnly: true
      volumes:
        - name: config
          configMap:
            name: filebeat-config
            defaultMode: 0640
        - name: data
          hostPath:
            path: /var/lib/filebeat-data-ruoyi
            type: DirectoryOrCreate
        - name: varlogpods
          hostPath:
            path: /var/log/pods
        - name: varlogcontainers
          hostPath:
            path: /var/log/containers
        - name: varlibdockercontainers
          hostPath:
            path: /var/lib/docker/containers
---
# ==================== 自监控 Service ====================
apiVersion: v1
kind: Service
metadata:
  name: ruoyi-filebeat-metrics
  namespace: ruoyi-logging
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: filebeat
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
spec:
  clusterIP: None
  ports:
    - name: metrics
      port: 5066
      targetPort: 5066
  selector:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: filebeat
```

**ES 摄取管道**（`ruoyi-log-pipeline`，用于归一化与丢弃）：

```bash
ES=ruoyi-elasticsearch.ruoyi-logging.svc.cluster.local:9200
curl -s -X PUT "http://$ES/_ingest/pipeline/ruoyi-log-pipeline" \
  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{
  "description": "RuoYi 日志归一化管道",
  "processors": [
    { "set": { "field": "ingest_time", "value": "{{{_ingest.timestamp}}}" } },
    { "remove": { "field": ["agent.hostname","ecs.version","event.original","host.os"], "ignore_missing": true } },
    { "gsub": { "field": "message", "pattern": "\\u001b\\[[0-9;]*m", "replacement": "" } },
    { "lowercase": { "field": "kubernetes.labels.app_kubernetes_io_name", "ignore_missing": true } },
    { "script": {
        "lang": "painless",
        "if": "ctx.kubernetes?.labels?.app_kubernetes_io_name != null",
        "source": "ctx.app = ctx.kubernetes.labels.app_kubernetes_io_name; ctx.service = ctx.app;"
    }},
    { "date": { "field": "time", "formats": ["ISO8601","yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss.SSS","UNIX_MS"], "target_field": "@timestamp", "ignore_failure": true } }
  ]
}' | jq

# 验证管道
curl -s -X POST "http://$ES/_ingest/pipeline/ruoyi-log-pipeline/_simulate" \
  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{
  "docs":[{"_source":{"message":"{\"time\":\"2025-01-01 10:00:00.123\",\"level\":\"ERROR\",\"tid\":\"abc.def\",\"message\":\"boom\"}","kubernetes":{"labels":{"app_kubernetes_io_name":"ruoyi-gateway"}}}}]
}' | jq '.docs[0].doc._source'
# 预期: 输出含 level=ERROR、trace_id 归一化、app=ruoyi-gateway
```

**配置校验（上线前必做）**：

```bash
# 1) filebeat.yml 语法校验
kubectl apply -f deploy/k8s/47-filebeat.yaml
POD=$(kubectl -n ruoyi-logging get pod -l app.kubernetes.io/name=filebeat -o jsonpath='{.items[0].metadata.name}')
kubectl -n ruoyi-logging exec $POD -- filebeat test config -c /etc/filebeat.yml
# 预期: Config OK

# 2) ES 连通性与输出配置校验
kubectl -n ruoyi-logging exec $POD -- filebeat test output -c /etc/filebeat.yml
# 预期: 
#   elasticsearch: http://...:9200...
#     parse url... OK
#     connection... OK
#     talk to server... OK
#     version: 7.17.21

# 3) 模块/模板校验（若启用）
kubectl -n ruoyi-logging exec $POD -- filebeat test config -e -c /etc/filebeat.yml 2>&1 | tail -5

# 4) 运行状态
kubectl -n ruoyi-logging get pods -l app.kubernetes.io/name=filebeat -o wide
# 预期: Pod 数 = 节点数（9 个），全部 Running（或部分因节点不可调度而少）
kubectl -n ruoyi-logging logs -l app.kubernetes.io/name=filebeat --tail=50 | grep -i -E 'error|warn|harvester'
# 预期: 无持续性 error；有 harvester started 记录
```

失败判据：

| 现象 | 原因 | 处理 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `Config OK` 报 `missing field` | yaml 缩进/字段名错 | 对照官方 filebeat.reference.yml |
| `talk to server... ERROR` | ES 地址/端口错或白名单 | 从 Pod 内 `curl http://ES:9200` 验证 |
| 无日志写入 ES | 索引模式/权限问题 | 检查 ILM alias 与 `index: ruoyi-logs` |
| `harvester` 报 `file not found` | 挂载路径错 | 确认 `/var/log/pods` 已挂载 |
| ES 中日志重复 | 多个 Filebeat 实例采集同一文件 | 检查 DaemonSet 是否意外多副本；registry 路径需每节点独立 |
| `data` 目录权限错误 | hostPath 权限 | 检查节点 `/var/lib/filebeat-data-ruoyi` 属主 |

#### 6.4.5 日志字段规范

| 字段 | 类型 | 来源 | 用途 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `@timestamp` | date | Filebeat 采集时间（或日志内 time） | 时间轴 |
| `time` | date | 应用日志时间 | 精确时间（用于核对时区） |
| `level` | keyword | 应用日志 level（ERROR/WARN/INFO） | 按级别筛选 |
| `logger_name` | keyword | 类名 | 定位代码 |
| `message` | text | 日志正文 | 全文检索 |
| `stack_trace` | text | 多行合并的异常栈 | 异常分析 |
| `trace_id` | keyword | 日志 MDC 中的 tid | **与 SkyWalking Trace 关联** |
| `thread_name` | keyword | 线程名 | 并发问题排查 |
| `app` | keyword | K8s 标签 `app_kubernetes_io_name` | 按服务筛选 |
| `kubernetes.namespace` | keyword | K8s 元数据 | 命名空间隔离 |
| `kubernetes.pod` | keyword | K8s 元数据 | 按实例定位 |
| `kubernetes.node` | keyword | K8s 元数据 | 定位节点 |
| `kubernetes.labels.topology_kubernetes_io_zone` | keyword | K8s 标签 | 可用区分析 |
| `tags` | keyword | Filebeat 处理 | `has_stacktrace` / `error` 快速标记 |
| `cluster` / `region` | keyword | add_fields | 多集群聚合 |

**Kibana 保存搜索（建议创建）**：

| 名称 | KQL 查询 |
| --- | --- |
| 所有错误日志 | `level:ERROR or level:FATAL` |
| 网关错误 | `app:ruoyi-gateway and level:ERROR` |
| 含异常栈 | `tags:has_stacktrace` |
| 按 trace 关联 | `trace_id:"<traceId>"`（可跨服务） |
| 登录失败 | `app:ruoyi-auth and message:*认证失败*` |
| 慢请求（Nginx） | `app:ruoyi-nginx and request_time > 2` |
| 定时任务 | `app:ruoyi-modules-job` |
| 某节点日志 | `kubernetes.node:"cn-hangzhou.10.1.1.5"` |

#### 6.4.6 验证日志端到端（含 traceId 关联）

```bash
# ---------- 1) 应用打一条带 traceId 的日志 ----------
# 方式一：触发业务异常（如错误密码登录）
curl -s -X POST https://api.ruoyi.example.com/auth/login \
  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -d '{"username":"admin","password":"wrong-password-xyz","code":"1234","uuid":"test-uuid-e2e"}'
# 预期: 返回认证失败（此操作会在 auth 服务产生 WARN/ERROR 日志）

# ---------- 2) 确认 Filebeat 采集到 ----------
kubectl -n ruoyi-logging logs -l app.kubernetes.io/name=filebeat --tail=100 | grep -i 'harvester started' | tail -3

# ---------- 3) 在 ES 中查询（等待 10-30 秒）----------
ES=ruoyi-elasticsearch.ruoyi-logging.svc.cluster.local:9200
curl -s "http://$ES/ruoyi-logs/_search?pretty" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{
  "size": 3,
  "sort": [{"@timestamp": "desc"}],
  "query": {
    "bool": {
      "filter": [
        {"term": {"app": "ruoyi-auth"}},
        {"range": {"@timestamp": {"gte": "now-10m"}}}
      ]
    }
  },
  "_source": ["@timestamp","level","app","message","trace_id","kubernetes.pod","kubernetes.namespace"]
}'
# 预期: 返回 hits，字段含 app=ruoyi-auth、kubernetes.namespace=ruoyi-prod、trace_id 非空

# ---------- 4) 按 traceId 跨服务关联 ----------
TRACE_ID=$(curl -s "http://$ES/ruoyi-logs/_search" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{
  "size":1,"sort":[{"@timestamp":"desc"}],
  "query":{"bool":{"filter":[{"exists":{"field":"trace_id"}},{"range":{"@timestamp":{"gte":"now-30m"}}}]}}
}' | jq -r '.hits.hits[0]._source.trace_id')
echo "trace_id = $TRACE_ID"

curl -s "http://$ES/ruoyi-logs/_search" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d "{
  \"size\": 20,
  \"sort\": [{\"@timestamp\": \"asc\"}],
  \"query\": {\"term\": {\"trace_id\": \"$TRACE_ID\"}},
  \"aggs\": {\"by_app\": {\"terms\": {\"field\": \"app\"}}}
}" | jq '{total:.hits.total.value, apps:[.aggregations.by_app.buckets[].key], timeline:[.hits.hits[]._source|{t:.@timestamp,app,level,msg:(.message|.[0:60])}]}'
# 预期: total >= 2，apps 含 ruoyi-gateway 与 ruoyi-auth（证明 traceId 贯穿）
```

**端到端通过标准**：
1. ES 中能查到近 10 分钟的应用日志，字段完整（`app`/`level`/`kubernetes.*` 均非空）；
2. `trace_id` 字段存在，且可在多个服务间检索到同一 traceId（证明日志与链路可关联）；
3. SkyWalking UI 中用同一 traceId 能查到对应 Trace；
4. Kibana 中创建 Index Pattern 后，Discover 页面可按 `level:ERROR` 过滤并看到 `stack_trace` 多行合并完整（不碎片化）。

### 6.5 告警通道与分级

#### 6.5.1 Alertmanager 配置

`deploy/k8s/48-alertmanager-config.yaml`（作为 `kube-prometheus-stack` 的 `alertmanager.config` 或独立 Secret `alertmanager-kube-prometheus-stack-alertmanager`）：

```yaml
global:
  resolve_timeout: 5m
  smtp_smarthost: "<SMTP_HOST>:465"
  smtp_from: "ruoyi-alert@example.com"
  smtp_auth_username: "<SMTP_USER>"
  smtp_auth_password: "<SMTP_PASSWORD>"
  smtp_require_tls: true

# ==================== 模板 ====================
templates:
  - /etc/alertmanager/templates/*.tmpl

# ==================== 路由树 ====================
route:
  receiver: "default"
  group_by: ["alertname", "namespace", "service"]
  group_wait: 30s
  group_interval: 5m
  repeat_interval: 4h
  # 关键：按 severity 分流
  routes:
    # ---------- P0：电话 + 短信 + 钉钉 + 企业微信 ----------
    - matchers: [ 'severity="P0"' ]
      receiver: "p0-oncall"
      group_wait: 10s
      group_interval: 1m
      repeat_interval: 15m
      continue: true
    # ---------- P1：钉钉 + 企业微信 + 邮件 ----------
    - matchers: [ 'severity="P1"' ]
      receiver: "p1-team"
      group_wait: 30s
      group_interval: 3m
      repeat_interval: 1h
      continue: true
    # ---------- P2：企业微信 + 邮件 ----------
    - matchers: [ 'severity="P2"' ]
      receiver: "p2-team"
      group_wait: 1m
      group_interval: 10m
      repeat_interval: 6h
      continue: true
    # ---------- P3：仅邮件/日报 ----------
    - matchers: [ 'severity="P3"' ]
      receiver: "p3-digest"
      group_wait: 5m
      group_interval: 30m
      repeat_interval: 24h
    # ---------- 业务告警（按命名空间细分）----------
    - matchers: [ 'namespace="ruoyi-prod"' ]
      receiver: "p1-team"
      continue: false
    - matchers: [ 'team="dba"' ]
      receiver: "p1-team"

# ==================== 抑制规则 ====================
inhibit_rules:
  # 节点宕机时，抑制该节点上所有 Pod 的告警（避免告警风暴）
  - source_matchers: [ 'alertname="RuoyiNodeNotReady"' ]
    target_matchers: [ 'alertname=~"Ruoyi.*"' ]
    equal: ["instance"]
  # 集群级故障抑制组件级告警
  - source_matchers: [ 'severity="P0"' ]
    target_matchers: [ 'severity=~"P1|P2|P3"' ]
    equal: ["alertname", "namespace"]
  # 网关 5xx 高时抑制下游服务告警（避免根因淹没）
  - source_matchers: [ 'alertname="RuoyiHighErrorRate", service="ruoyi-gateway"' ]
    target_matchers: [ 'alertname="RuoyiHighErrorRate"' ]
    equal: ["namespace"]
  # ES 集群故障时抑制 Kibana/SkyWalking 告警
  - source_matchers: [ 'alertname="RuoyiEsClusterNotGreen"' ]
    target_matchers: [ 'alertname=~"RuoyiEsUnassignedShards|RuoyiEsJvmHeapHigh"' ]
  # 维护窗口（通过 silence 实现，此处为示例）
  - source_matchers: [ 'alertname="MaintenanceWindow"' ]
    target_matchers: [ 'severity=~".*"' ]

# ==================== 接收器 ====================
receivers:
  - name: "default"
    webhook_configs:
      - url: "<ALERT_WEBHOOK_WECOM>"
        send_resolved: true

  # ---------- P0：电话/短信（阿里云语音+短信 via webhook 网关）----------
  - name: "p0-oncall"
    webhook_configs:
      - url: "http://ruoyi-alert-gateway.ruoyi-monitoring.svc.cluster.local:8080/p0"
        send_resolved: true
        max_alerts: 0
    dingtalk_configs: []     # 需 alertmanager-webhook-dingtalk 或自定义 webhook
    email_configs:
      - to: "oncall-p0@example.com"
        send_resolved: true
        headers:
          Subject: '[P0-紧急] {{ template "ruoyi.subject" . }}'
        html: '{{ template "ruoyi.html" . }}'

  # ---------- P1 ----------
  - name: "p1-team"
    webhook_configs:
      - url: "<ALERT_WEBHOOK_DINGTALK>"
        send_resolved: true
      - url: "<ALERT_WEBHOOK_WECOM>"
        send_resolved: true
    email_configs:
      - to: "devops@example.com,sre@example.com"
        send_resolved: true
        headers:
          Subject: '[P1-重要] {{ template "ruoyi.subject" . }}'
        html: '{{ template "ruoyi.html" . }}'

  # ---------- P2 ----------
  - name: "p2-team"
    webhook_configs:
      - url: "<ALERT_WEBHOOK_WECOM>"
        send_resolved: true
    email_configs:
      - to: "devops@example.com"
        send_resolved: true
        headers:
          Subject: '[P2-一般] {{ template "ruoyi.subject" . }}'

  # ---------- P3：日报汇总（可由外部脚本每日拉取 API 汇总）----------
  - name: "p3-digest"
    webhook_configs:
      - url: "<ALERT_WEBHOOK_WECOM>"
        send_resolved: false
```

**钉钉/企业微信消息模板**（`deploy/k8s/alert-templates.tmpl`）：

```gotemplate
{{ define "ruoyi.subject" }}[{{ .Status | toUpper }}{{ if eq .Status "firing" }}:{{ .Alerts.Firing | len }}{{ end }}] {{ .CommonLabels.alertname }} ({{ .CommonLabels.severity }}){{ end }}

{{ define "ruoyi.html" }}
<!DOCTYPE html><html><body style="font-family:Microsoft YaHei,Arial,sans-serif;font-size:14px">
<h3 style="color:{{ if eq .Status "firing" }}#d93025{{ else }}#188038{{ end }}">
  {{ if eq .Status "firing" }}🔴 告警触发{{ else }}✅ 告警恢复{{ end }} — {{ .CommonLabels.alertname }}
</h3>
<table border="1" cellpadding="6" cellspacing="0" style="border-collapse:collapse">
  <tr><td><b>级别</b></td><td>{{ .CommonLabels.severity }}</td></tr>
  <tr><td><b>集群</b></td><td>{{ .CommonLabels.cluster }}</td></tr>
  <tr><td><b>命名空间</b></td><td>{{ .CommonLabels.namespace }}</td></tr>
  <tr><td><b>服务</b></td><td>{{ .CommonLabels.service }}</td></tr>
  <tr><td><b>开始时间</b></td><td>{{ (index .Alerts 0).StartsAt.Format "2006-01-02 15:04:05" }}</td></tr>
</table>
{{ range .Alerts }}
<p><b>摘要：</b>{{ .Annotations.summary }}<br/>
   <b>详情：</b>{{ .Annotations.description }}<br/>
   <b>运行手册：</b><a href="{{ .Annotations.runbook }}">{{ .Annotations.runbook }}</a></p>
{{ end }}
<hr/><p style="color:#666;font-size:12px">来源：若依生产监控告警系统 | 集群 {{ .CommonLabels.cluster }}</p>
</body></html>
{{ end }}

{{ define "ruoyi.dingtalk" }}
{{- range .Alerts -}}
### {{ if eq .Status "firing" }}🔴 告警{{ else }}✅ 恢复{{ end }}：{{ .Labels.alertname }}
- **级别**：{{ .Labels.severity }}
- **服务**：{{ .Labels.service }}
- **命名空间**：{{ .Labels.namespace }}
- **摘要**：{{ .Annotations.summary }}
- **时间**：{{ .StartsAt.Format "2006-01-02 15:04:05" }}
{{ end -}}
{{ end }}
```

应用配置：

```bash
kubectl -n ruoyi-monitoring create secret generic alertmanager-kube-prometheus-stack-alertmanager \
  --from-file=alertmanager.yaml=/dev/stdin <<'EOF'
# 粘贴上面的 alertmanager 配置（去掉 global.headers 中的非法字段）
EOF

kubectl -n ruoyi-monitoring create configmap alert-templates \
  --from-file=ruoyi.tmpl=deploy/k8s/alert-templates.tmpl

# 让 Alertmanager 挂载模板（需在 helm values 中增加）
# alertmanager.alertmanagerSpec.volumes / volumeMounts：
#   - name: templates
#     configMap: { name: alert-templates }
#   挂载到 /etc/alertmanager/templates

kubectl -n ruoyi-monitoring rollout restart statefulset alertmanager-kube-prometheus-stack-alertmanager
kubectl -n ruoyi-monitoring logs -l app.kubernetes.io/name=alertmanager --tail=50 | grep -i -E 'error|completed loading'
# 预期: "Completed loading of configuration file" 且无 error

# 发送测试告警
kubectl -n ruoyi-monitoring port-forward svc/kube-prometheus-stack-alertmanager 19093:9093 &
curl -s -X POST http://127.0.0.1:19093/api/v2/alerts -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '[{
  "labels":{"alertname":"TestAlert","severity":"P2","namespace":"ruoyi-prod","service":"ruoyi-gateway","cluster":"ack-ruoyi-prod"},
  "annotations":{"summary":"这是一条测试告警","description":"验证告警通道连通性","runbook":"https://wiki.example.com/runbook/test"},
  "startsAt":"'"$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)"'"
}]'
sleep 20
# 预期: 钉钉/企业微信群收到测试告警
kill %1
```

失败判据：

| 现象 | 原因 | 处理 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Alertmanager 启动失败 | 配置 YAML 语法错 | `kubectl logs` 查看具体行号；用 `amtool check-config` 校验 |
| 钉钉未收到 | Webhook 被限流（20 条/分钟）或关键词不匹配 | 钉钉机器人需含关键字（如"告警"）；建议用 `alertmanager-webhook-dingtalk` 转换 |
| 企业微信未收到 | markdown 格式错误 | 用 text 类型或校验 markdown 语法 |
| 邮件未发出 | SMTP 端口/TLS 配置错 | 465 用 SSL；587 用 STARTTLS；检查 `smtp_require_tls` |
| 重复告警过多 | `repeat_interval` 太短 | 按级别调整（P3 调至 24h） |
| 告警风暴 | 缺少 inhibit_rules | 按 6.5.1 抑制规则补充 |

#### 6.5.2 告警分级定义表（P0–P3）

| 级别 | 名称 | 判定标准 | 响应时限 | 通知渠道 | 升级路径 | 示例 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **P0** | 灾难级 | 核心业务完全不可用；数据丢失风险；安全事件 | **5 分钟**内响应 | 电话 + 短信 + 钉钉 + 企业微信 + 邮件 | 值班 → SRE 负责人（15min）→ 技术总监（30min）→ CTO（60min） | 全部网关 Pod 挂；RDS 主库宕机且无法切换；集群不可用 |
| **P1** | 严重级 | 核心功能受损；可用性 <99%；关键依赖单点故障 | **15 分钟**内响应 | 钉钉 + 企业微信 + 邮件 | 值班 → SRE 负责人（30min）→ 项目经理（2h） | 单可用区故障；Nacos 多数派丢失；ES 集群 red；5xx>1% |
| **P2** | 一般级 | 非核心功能异常；资源接近瓶颈；性能下降 | **2 小时**内响应 | 企业微信 + 邮件 | 值班 → 当班工程师（4h）→ 迭代排期 | Pod 频繁重启；CPU 限流；堆内存 >85%；PVC >85% |
| **P3** | 提示级 | 潜在风险；趋势性劣化；需优化项 | **1 个工作日**内处理 | 邮件/日报 | 迭代排期 | 慢查询增多；PDB 阻塞；证书 30 天内过期；磁盘 80% |

**阈值表（与 6.3.4 规则对应）**：

| 指标 | P0 | P1 | P2 | P3 | 持续时长 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 服务 5xx 比例 | >50% | >5% | >1% | — | 1m / 2m / 5m |
| 接口 P99 | >10s | >5s | >2s | >1s | 2m / 5m / 10m |
| 可用副本数 | =0 | <50% | <期望 | — | 1m / 5m / 10m |
| 网关 QPS（超阈值） | >5000 | >4000 | >3000 | >2000 | 5m |
| CPU 限流比例 | — | — | >25% | >10% | 10m / 15m |
| JVM 堆使用率 | — | >95% | >85% | >75% | 5m / 10m / 30m |
| GC 平均停顿 | — | — | >500ms | >200ms | 10m / 30m |
| 节点 CPU | — | >95% | >85% | >70% | 10m / 15m / 30m |
| 节点内存 | >95% | >90% | >80% | — | 5m / 15m / 30m |
| 磁盘使用率 | >95% | >90% | >85% | >80% | 5m / 15m / 30m |
| ES 集群状态 | red | yellow>10m | unassigned>0 | — | 1m / 10m / 10m |
| ES 堆使用率 | — | >90% | >85% | >75% | 5m / 10m / 30m |
| MySQL 连接数 | — | >95% | >80% | >70% | 5m / 10m / 30m |
| Nacos 节点数 | <2 | =2 | — | — | 1m / 5m |
| 证书剩余天数 | <7 | <15 | <30 | <60 | 1h |
| Pod 重启次数 | — | — | >2 次/10m | >1 次/30m | 5m / 30m |

#### 6.5.3 值班与升级路径

| 时段 | 值班角色 | 职责 | 联系方式 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 工作日 09:00–18:00 | 一线（DevOps 工程师） | 告警确认、初步处置、按手册操作 | 企业微信 + 电话 |
| 工作日 18:00–次日 09:00 | 二线（SRE 轮值） | 夜间告警处置，P0/P1 可越权执行预案 | 电话（值班表） |
| 周末/节假日 | 二线轮值（12 小时班） | 同上 | 电话 |
| 全天 | 三线（架构师/负责人） | P0/P1 决策、跨团队协调 | 电话 |
| 全天 | DBA（值班） | 数据库相关 P0/P1 | 电话 |
| 全天 | 开发负责人（值班） | 代码缺陷定位、紧急热修 | 电话 |

**升级路径（严格按时限自动升级）**：

```text
P0：告警触发 → 一线（5min 未响应）→ 二线+SRE负责人（15min）→ 技术总监（30min）→ CTO（60min）
P1：告警触发 → 一线（15min）→ 二线（30min）→ 项目经理（2h）→ 技术总监（4h）
P2：告警触发 → 一线（2h）→ 当班工程师（4h）→ 迭代排期
P3：告警触发 → 日报汇总 → 迭代排期
```

**告警认领与记录要求**：
1. 所有 P0/P1 告警必须在 30 分钟内创建故障工单（含时间线）；
2. 处置过程按《03-故障处理手册》对应章节执行；
3. 恢复后 24 小时内提交复盘报告（含根因、影响、改进项）；
4. P0 故障必须归档为案例，纳入知识库。

#### 6.5.4 静默/抑制与降噪

**静默（Silence）操作**：

```bash
AM=kube-prometheus-stack-alertmanager.ruoyi-monitoring.svc.cluster.local:9093

# 创建维护窗口静默（例如发版期间静默 P2/P3）
curl -s -X POST "http://$AM/api/v2/silences" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{
  "matchers": [
    {"name":"severity","value":"P2|P3","isRegex":true,"isEqual":true},
    {"name":"namespace","value":"ruoyi-prod","isRegex":false,"isEqual":true}
  ],
  "startsAt": "'"$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)"'",
  "endsAt": "'"$(date -u -d '+2 hours' +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)"'",
  "createdBy": "devops",
  "comment": "CHG-2025-0118 发版维护窗口"
}' | jq -r '.silenceID'
# 预期: 返回 silenceID（记录到变更单）

# 查看当前静默
curl -s "http://$AM/api/v2/silences" | jq '.[]|{id,status:.status.state,comment,endsAt}'

# 结束静默
curl -s -X DELETE "http://$AM/api/v2/silence/<SILENCE_ID>"
```

**降噪措施清单**：

| 措施 | 实现 | 效果 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 合理分组 | `group_by: [alertname, namespace, service]` | 同类告警合并为一条 |
| 抑制规则 | `inhibit_rules`（节点级抑制 Pod 级） | 节点故障时告警量降 90% |
| 分级路由 | 按 severity 分流不同渠道 | P3 不打扰值班 |
| 延长 repeat | P3 `repeat_interval: 24h` | 减少重复推送 |
| 阈值调优 | 基于历史数据把 P2 阈值上调 10% | 减少边界抖动 |
| 静默窗口 | 发版/演练期间创建 Silence | 计划性噪声归零 |
| 丢弃噪声日志 | Filebeat `drop_event`（health/metrics） | 减少 ES 写入 30% |
| 探针调整 | 探针路径指向轻量端点（`/actuator/health/liveness`） | 减少日志与指标噪声 |
| 自愈优先 | 见 6.5.5 | 一部分告警自动修复，无需人工 |

**告警质量月度评审**：每月统计告警条数、误报率、平均响应时间，目标：误报率 <10%，P1 以上平均响应 <15 分钟。

#### 6.5.5 告警自愈与降噪

**自愈场景表**：

| 场景 | 触发条件 | 自愈动作 | 实现方式 | 风险 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Pod 僵死（探针失败） | livenessProbe 失败 | K8s 自动重启容器 | 探针 + `restartPolicy: Always` | 无 |
| 节点 NotReady | NPD 检测 | 节点自愈（ACK 重建节点） | 节点池 `auto_repair: true` | 需 PDB 保护 |
| 副本不足 | HPA 检测 | 自动扩容 | HPA + cluster-autoscaler | 成本上升 |
| PVC 满 | 使用率 >85% | 自动扩容 PVC | 脚本 + `allowVolumeExpansion: true` | 需文件系统支持在线扩容 |
| 配置错误 | 探测失败 | 自动回滚 Deployment | 脚本监听 + `kubectl rollout undo` | 需谨慎 |
| 磁盘满（ES 索引只读） | flood_stage 触发 | 清理 30 天前索引 | CronJob | 数据保留策略 |
| 证书即将过期 | <30 天 | cert-manager 自动续期 | cert-manager | 无 |
| Nacos 节点宕机 | Pod 异常 | K8s 重建 Pod，JRaft 自动选主 | StatefulSet | 无 |

**自愈 CronJob 示例（PVC 自动扩容）**：

```yaml
apiVersion: batch/v1
kind: CronJob
metadata:
  name: ruoyi-pvc-autoresize
  namespace: ruoyi-monitoring
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: ruoyi-pvc-autoresize
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: ruoyi-cloud
spec:
  schedule: "*/30 * * * *"
  concurrencyPolicy: Forbid
  jobTemplate:
    spec:
      template:
        spec:
          restartPolicy: OnFailure
          serviceAccountName: ruoyi-autoresize
          containers:
            - name: autoresize
              image: <YOUR_ACR_REGISTRY>/ruoyi-prod/rocky-tools:9.3
              command: ["/bin/sh", "-c"]
              args:
                - |
                  set -eu
                  # 查询使用率 > 85% 的 PVC 并扩容 20%
                  for ns in ruoyi-prod ruoyi-middleware ruoyi-monitoring ruoyi-logging; do
                    for pvc in $(kubectl -n $ns get pvc -o jsonpath='{.items[*].metadata.name}'); do
                      used=$(kubectl -n $ns get --raw "/api/v1/namespaces/$ns/persistentvolumeclaims/$pvc" 2>/dev/null | jq -r '.status.capacity.storage // "0Gi"' | sed 's/Gi//')
                      [ "$used" = "null" ] && continue
                      # 使用 metrics 更准确（kubelet_volume_stats_used_bytes），此处简化为容量阈值检查
                      size=$(kubectl -n $ns get pvc $pvc -o jsonpath='{.spec.resources.requests.storage}' | sed 's/Gi//')
                      if [ "$size" -lt 200 ]; then
                        new=$(( size * 12 / 10 ))
                        echo "resizing $ns/$pvc: ${size}Gi -> ${new}Gi"
                        kubectl -n $ns patch pvc $pvc -p "{\"spec\":{\"resources\":{\"requests\":{\"storage\":\"${new}Gi\"}}}}"
                      fi
                    done
                  done
```

> **警告**：自动扩 PVC 有风险（云盘有配额、文件系统需支持在线扩容、扩容不可逆）。**建议先人工确认**，或在非核心命名空间试点。生产建议改为"告警 + 人工确认"。

### 6.6 阿里云侧可观测

#### 6.6.1 ARMS 应用监控接入

**方式一：ACK 一键接入（推荐）**

```text
控制台：ACK → 集群 → 运维管理 → Prometheus 监控 / ARMS 应用监控 → 一键接入
  - 选择命名空间：ruoyi-prod
  - 选择 Java 应用自动注入探针（需重启 Pod）
  - 选择「Java 应用监控」+「应用安全」按需
```

**方式二：手动接入（Java Agent）**

```yaml
# 在 Deployment 中增加 ARMS 探针 initContainer（与 SkyWalking 可共存，但建议二选一以免性能开销翻倍）
      initContainers:
        - name: arms-agent
          image: registry.cn-hangzhou.aliyuncs.com/arms-docker/arms-agent:latest
          command: ["/bin/sh","-c","cp -r /opt/arms/* /arms/ || true"]
          volumeMounts:
```
